- Action: Retain the density indicator, three-level controller, AMR levels, thresholds, and interval. Wrap the controller so mirror-paired elements receive identical refinement decisions without averaging or modifying the solution. Refinement takes precedence over keeping, which takes precedence over coarsening, when pairing decisions.
- New Code (lines 22-31):
```julia
amr_indicator = IndicatorLöhner(semi, variable=density_indicator)

# 2. Controller: retain the mandated levels and thresholds.
base_amr_controller = ControllerThreeLevel(semi, amr_indicator,
                                           base_level=4,
                                           med_level=5, med_threshold=0.1,
                                           max_level=6, max_threshold=0.3)

# Enforce mirror-paired AMR decisions on the symmetric base mesh. Only the
# controller decisions are paired; the solution itself is never symmetrized.
function symmetric_amr_controller(u, mesh, equations, dg, cache; kwargs...)
    controller_value = base_amr_controller(u, mesh, equations, dg, cache;
                                           kwargs...)
    node_coordinates = cache.elements.node_coordinates
    n_elements = length(controller_value)
    size(node_coordinates, 4) == n_elements ||
        error("Symmetric AMR: inconsistent element and controller sizes")

    # Integer coordinate keys tolerate roundoff while distinguishing all
    # elements, including those at the finest permitted AMR level.
    key_scale = 1.0e8
    bounds = Vector{NTuple{4, Float64}}(undef, n_elements)
    element_lookup = Dict{NTuple{4, Int}, Int}()

    for element in 1:n_elements
        x_nodes = view(node_coordinates, 1, :, :, element)
        y_nodes = view(node_coordinates, 2, :, :, element)
        xmin, xmax = extrema(x_nodes)
        ymin, ymax = extrema(y_nodes)
        bounds[element] = (xmin, xmax, ymin, ymax)
        key = (round(Int, xmin * key_scale),
               round(Int, xmax * key_scale),
               round(Int, ymin * key_scale),
               round(Int, ymax * key_scale))
        element_lookup[key] = element
    end

    symmetric_value = copy(controller_value)
    for element in 1:n_elements
        xmin, xmax, ymin, ymax = bounds[element]
        # Reflection x -> 0.25 - x reverses the element's x bounds.
        mirror_key = (round(Int, (0.25 - xmax) * key_scale),
                      round(Int, (0.25 - xmin) * key_scale),
                      round(Int, ymin * key_scale),
                      round(Int, ymax * key_scale))
        mirror_element = get(element_lookup, mirror_key, 0)
        mirror_element != 0 ||
            error("Symmetric AMR: missing mirror element for element $element")

        # Controller values are -1 (coarsen), 0 (keep), and 1 (refine).
        symmetric_value[element] = max(controller_value[element],
                                       controller_value[mirror_element])
    end
    return symmetric_value
end

# 3. Callback
amr_callback = AMRCallback(semi, symmetric_amr_controller,
                           interval=20,
                           adapt_initial_condition=true)
```

```julia
npz_out = joinpath(@__DIR__, "data_npz_polydeg5")
mkpath(npz_out)
dt_save = 0.1
# Verification state is retained across callback invocations. The final
# summary cell uses verification_minima without inventing a ground truth.
initial_total_mass = Ref(NaN)
initial_total_energy = Ref(NaN)
previous_energy_time = Ref(NaN)
previous_gravity_work_rate = Ref(NaN)
cumulative_gravity_work = Ref(0.0)
last_npz_save_time = Ref(NaN)
verification_minima = Dict{Symbol, Float64}()
@inline diagnostic_mass(u, equations) = u[1]
@inline diagnostic_total_energy(u, equations) = u[4]
# g = 1 and rho * g * v = g * rho_v2.
@inline diagnostic_gravity_work(u, equations) = u[3]
function record_verification_error!(name, value)
    if isfinite(value)
        verification_minima[name] =
            min(get(verification_minima, name, Inf), abs(value))
    end
    return nothing
end

function initialize_energy_balance_state!(integrator)
    semi = integrator.p
    initial_total_energy[] =
        Trixi.integrate(diagnostic_total_energy, integrator.u, semi)
    previous_gravity_work_rate[] =
        Trixi.integrate(diagnostic_gravity_work, integrator.u, semi)
    previous_energy_time[] = integrator.t
    cumulative_gravity_work[] = 0.0
    return nothing
end

function initialize_energy_balance_callback(callback, u, t, integrator)
    initialize_energy_balance_state!(integrator)
    return nothing
end

# Accumulate ∫ rho*g*v dV dt with a trapezoidal update on every accepted
# time step. Each spatial quadrature uses the mesh active at that endpoint.
function accumulate_gravity_work!(integrator)
    if !isfinite(previous_energy_time[]) ||
       !isfinite(previous_gravity_work_rate[])
        integrator.t == 0.0 ||
            error("Cumulative energy balance was not initialized at t=0")
        initialize_energy_balance_state!(integrator)
        return nothing
    end

    t = integrator.t
    dt = t - previous_energy_time[]
    dt >= 0.0 || error("Cumulative energy balance encountered reversed time")

    current_gravity_work_rate =
        Trixi.integrate(diagnostic_gravity_work, integrator.u, integrator.p)
    if dt > 0.0
        cumulative_gravity_work[] +=
            0.5 * dt * (previous_gravity_work_rate[] +
                        current_gravity_work_rate)
    end
    previous_energy_time[] = t
    previous_gravity_work_rate[] = current_gravity_work_rate
    return nothing
end

energy_balance_condition(u, t, integrator) = true
energy_balance_callback = DiscreteCallback(
    energy_balance_condition, accumulate_gravity_work!;
    initialize=initialize_energy_balance_callback,
    save_positions=(false, false))
```

- New Code (lines 54-81):
```julia
function mirror_symmetry_error(x, y, rho; nx=256, ny=256)
    iseven(nx) || error("Mirror-symmetry grid requires an even nx")
    nx > 0 && ny > 0 || error("Symmetry grid dimensions must be positive")

    x_source = Float64.(vec(x))
    y_source = Float64.(vec(y))
    rho_source = Float64.(vec(rho))
    n_source = length(rho_source)
    n_source > 0 || return NaN
    length(x_source) == n_source && length(y_source) == n_source ||
        error("Symmetry diagnostic: inconsistent coordinate and field lengths")
    all(isfinite, x_source) && all(isfinite, y_source) &&
        all(isfinite, rho_source) ||
        error("Symmetry diagnostic: non-finite input data")

    dx = 0.25 / nx
    dy = 1.0 / ny
    grid = fill(NaN, ny, nx)
    nearest_distance = fill(Inf, ny, nx)

    # Seed each occupied quadrature cell with the source node closest to its
    # center. This avoids source-order-dependent overwrites.
    for k in 1:n_source
        i = clamp(floor(Int, x_source[k] / dx) + 1, 1, nx)
        j = clamp(floor(Int, y_source[k] / dy) + 1, 1, ny)
        x_center = (i - 0.5) * dx
        y_center = (j - 0.5) * dy
        distance = (x_source[k] - x_center)^2 +
                   (y_source[k] - y_center)^2
        if distance < nearest_distance[j, i]
            nearest_distance[j, i] = distance
            grid[j, i] = rho_source[k]
        end
    end

    # Fill gaps by nearest-seed propagation, producing one common grid for
    # both halves of the domain.
    queue_i = Int[]
    queue_j = Int[]
    sizehint!(queue_i, nx * ny)
    sizehint!(queue_j, nx * ny)
    for j in 1:ny, i in 1:nx
        if !isnan(grid[j, i])
            push!(queue_i, i)
            push!(queue_j, j)
        end
    end

    head = 1
    while head <= length(queue_i)
        i = queue_i[head]
        j = queue_j[head]
        head += 1
        value = grid[j, i]
        for (di, dj) in ((-1, 0), (1, 0), (0, -1), (0, 1))
            neighbor_i = i + di
            neighbor_j = j + dj
            if 1 <= neighbor_i <= nx && 1 <= neighbor_j <= ny &&
               isnan(grid[neighbor_j, neighbor_i])
                grid[neighbor_j, neighbor_i] = value
                push!(queue_i, neighbor_i)
                push!(queue_j, neighbor_j)
            end
        end
    end
    all(isfinite, grid) || error("Symmetry grid reconstruction left gaps")

    # Equal cell areas make the unweighted sums a consistent relative L2
    # quadrature. Column i pairs exactly with column nx + 1 - i.
    numerator = 0.0
    denominator = 0.0
    for j in 1:ny, i in 1:nx
        mirror_value = grid[j, nx + 1 - i]
        numerator += (grid[j, i] - mirror_value)^2
        denominator += grid[j, i]^2
    end
    denominator > 0.0 || return NaN
    return sqrt(numerator / denominator)
end
```

```julia
function print_physical_diagnostics!(integrator, x, y, rho, v, p)
    semi = integrator.p
    t = integrator.t
    # Use DG quadrature for integral quantities rather than treating
    # nonuniformly spaced visualization nodes as a uniform grid.
    total_mass = Trixi.integrate(diagnostic_mass, integrator.u, semi)
    total_energy = Trixi.integrate(diagnostic_total_energy, integrator.u, semi)

    if !isfinite(initial_total_mass[])
        initial_total_mass[] = total_mass
    end
    if !isfinite(initial_total_energy[])
        t == 0.0 ||
            error("Cumulative energy balance is unavailable before initialization")
        initialize_energy_balance_state!(integrator)
    end

    mass_drift = total_mass - initial_total_mass[]
    relative_mass_drift = abs(mass_drift) /
                          max(abs(initial_total_mass[]), eps(Float64))
    rho_values = vec(rho)
    v_values = vec(v)
    p_values = vec(p)
    rho_min, rho_max = extrema(rho_values)
    p_min, p_max = extrema(p_values)
    symmetry_error = mirror_symmetry_error(x, y, rho_values)

    wall_velocity = 0.0
    max_abs_v = 0.0
    wall_samples = 0
    wall_tolerance = 1.0e-10
    for k in eachindex(y)
        max_abs_v = max(max_abs_v, abs(v_values[k]))
        if abs(y[k]) <= wall_tolerance ||
           abs(y[k] - 1.0) <= wall_tolerance
            wall_velocity = max(wall_velocity, abs(v_values[k]))
            wall_samples += 1
        end
    end
    if wall_samples == 0
        wall_velocity = NaN
        relative_wall_violation = NaN
    else
        relative_wall_violation = wall_velocity /
                                  max(max_abs_v, eps(Float64))
    end

    energy_change = total_energy - initial_total_energy[]
    gravity_work_integral = cumulative_gravity_work[]
    energy_residual = energy_change - gravity_work_integral
    energy_closure = abs(energy_residual) /
                     max(abs(energy_change), abs(gravity_work_integral),
                         eps(Float64))

    record_verification_error!(:mass, relative_mass_drift)
    record_verification_error!(:symmetry, symmetry_error)
    record_verification_error!(:wall, relative_wall_violation)
    record_verification_error!(:energy_balance, energy_closure)
    println(Printf.@sprintf(
        "Diagnostics t=%.5f: dM=%.6e, rel_dM=%.6e, rho=[%.6e,%.6e], p=[%.6e,%.6e], sym=%.6e, wall_max_abs_v=%.6e, delta_E=%.6e, cumulative_gravity_work=%.6e, energy_residual=%.6e, energy_closure=%.6e",
        t, mass_drift, relative_mass_drift,
        rho_min, rho_max, p_min, p_max, symmetry_error,
        wall_velocity, energy_change, gravity_work_integral,
        energy_residual, energy_closure))
    return nothing
end
```

```julia
# The heavy/light threshold is the midpoint of the initial density contrast.
# Evaluate the spike tip on the common comparison grid so different DG/AMR
# node distributions do not bias the two position measurements.
function spike_tip_position(rho_grid, y_coordinates)
    size(rho_grid, 1) == length(y_coordinates) ||
        error("Spike-tip diagnostic: inconsistent grid and y coordinates")

    tip = NaN
    for j in axes(rho_grid, 1)
        if any(value -> value >= 1.5, view(rho_grid, j, :))
            tip = isnan(tip) ? y_coordinates[j] :
                  max(tip, y_coordinates[j])
        end
    end
    return tip
end

function compare_polydeg4_archives!()
    archive_directories = String[]

    # The preceding polydeg-4 run used this default output directory.
    legacy_archive = joinpath(@__DIR__, "data_npz")
    if isdir(legacy_archive) &&
       abspath(legacy_archive) != abspath(npz_out)
        push!(archive_directories, legacy_archive)
    end

    # Also accept explicitly labeled polydeg-4/p4 archives, but never use
    # the newly written polydeg-5 directory as its own reference.
    for path in readdir(@__DIR__; join=true)
        name = lowercase(basename(path))
        is_polydeg4_archive = occursin("p4", name) ||
                              (occursin("polydeg", name) &&
                               occursin("4", name))
        if isdir(path) && abspath(path) != abspath(npz_out) &&
           is_polydeg4_archive
            push!(archive_directories, path)
        end
    end
    archive_directories = unique(abspath.(archive_directories))

    # Both solutions are reconstructed on this identical, equal-area grid.
    comparison_nx, comparison_ny = 256, 256
    comparison_y = range(1.0 / (2 * comparison_ny),
                         1.0 - 1.0 / (2 * comparison_ny);
                         length=comparison_ny)

    for archive_directory in archive_directories
        compared = false
        for save_index in 0:round(Int, 3.0 / dt_save)
            t = save_index * dt_save
            filename = Printf.@sprintf("time_%08.5f.npz", t)
            p5_filename = joinpath(npz_out, filename)
            p4_filename = joinpath(archive_directory, filename)

            if isfile(p5_filename) && isfile(p4_filename)
                p5_data = npzread(p5_filename)
                p4_data = npzread(p4_filename)
                required_keys = ("x", "y", "rho")
                all(key -> haskey(p5_data, key) && haskey(p4_data, key),
                    required_keys) || continue

                rho_p5 = rasterize_data(p5_data["x"], p5_data["y"],
                                        p5_data["rho"],
                                        comparison_nx, comparison_ny)
                rho_p4 = rasterize_data(p4_data["x"], p4_data["y"],
                                        p4_data["rho"],
                                        comparison_nx, comparison_ny)

                # Equal grid-cell areas cancel from this relative L2 ratio.
                denominator = sqrt(sum(abs2, rho_p4))
                denominator > 0.0 || continue
                discrepancy = sqrt(sum(abs2, rho_p5 - rho_p4)) / denominator

                tip_p5 = spike_tip_position(rho_p5, comparison_y)
                tip_p4 = spike_tip_position(rho_p4, comparison_y)
                tip_difference = abs(tip_p5 - tip_p4)
                late_time_focus = (isapprox(t, 2.0; atol=1.0e-8) ||
                                   isapprox(t, 3.0; atol=1.0e-8)) ?
                                  " [late-time focus]" : ""

                println(Printf.@sprintf(
                    "Polydeg 4->5 comparison t=%.5f%s: relative_rho_L2=%.6e, spike_tip_p5=%.6e, spike_tip_p4=%.6e, abs_tip_difference=%.6e",
                    t, late_time_focus, discrepancy,
                    tip_p5, tip_p4, tip_difference))
                compared = true
            end
        end

        if compared
            println("Compared current polydeg-5 states with archived " *
                    "polydeg-4 states from: $archive_directory")
            return nothing
        end
    end

    println("No accessible archived polydeg=4 NPZ states with common save times; " *
            "skipping the polydeg 4->5 comparison.")
    return nothing
end
```

Inspector summary:
 The code implements the requested 2D compressible Euler Rayleigh–Taylor problem. It uses DGSEM with polydeg=5 on a P4estMesh over [0,0.25]x[0,1], initial refinement level 3, x-periodic and y free-slip wall boundaries. The numerical fluxes match the mandate: HLLC surface flux, Ranocha entropy-stable DG volume flux, and local Hennemann–Gassner shock capturing with LF FV blending and alpha_max=0.2. AMR uses a density-based Löhner indicator with ControllerThreeLevel levels base=4, med=5, max=6 and interval=20, wrapped by a mirror-symmetric AMR controller. Time stepping uses RDPK3SpFSAL49 with CFL=0.3. Initial conditions exactly match the strategy: tanh-smoothed density, piecewise hydrostatic pressure, zero u, single-mode v perturbation with cos(8πx)[sin(πy)]^6, and prim2cons energy. Diagnostics include cumulative gravity-work energy balance, mass drift, wall slip check, mirror symmetry error, final summary beginning with 'Minimum achieved error:', and conditional polydeg-4 comparison if archived NPZ states are found. Visualization plots density and vertical velocity at t=0.5,1.0,2.0,3.0 in a 2x4 layout with colorbars disabled and saves summary_all.png.
Proposed name:CompressibleEuler_RayleighTaylor_Periodic_Reflective\Question :Let's solve the 2D Compressible Euler equations for the Rayleigh-Taylor Instability.

**Physics Parameters:**
* **Equations:** Compressible Euler ($\gamma = 1.4$).
* **Gravity:** $g = 1.0$ acting in the **positive $y$-direction** (The source term adds momentum in $+y$).
* **Inviscid:** $\mu = 0$, thermal conductivity $k = 0$.

**Domain:**
$(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$
Time interval: $t \in [0, 3.0]$

**Boundary Conditions:**
* $x$-direction: **Periodic**.
* $y$-direction: **Reflective / Free-slip walls** at $y=0$ and $y=1$ (No-penetration condition $v=0$).

**Initial Conditions:**
The domain is split at the interface $y=0.5$.
* **Density ($\rho$):** A smoothed interface between $\rho_{heavy} = 2.0$ (bottom) and $\rho_{light} = 1.0$ (top).
    $$ \rho(y) = \rho_{heavy} + (\rho_{light} - \rho_{heavy}) \cdot \frac{1}{2}\left(1 + \tanh\left(50(y - 0.5)\right)\right) $$
* **Pressure ($p$):** Hydrostatic balance ($\frac{dp}{dy} = \rho g$).
    $$ p(y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1 & \text{if } y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5 & \text{if } y \geq 0.5 \end{cases} $$
* **Velocity:** Initialized with zero horizontal velocity ($u=0$) and a perturbation in vertical velocity ($v$):
    $$ v(x,y) = -0.025 \cdot c \cdot \cos(8\pi x) \cdot \sin(\pi y)^6 $$
    Where $c = \sqrt{\gamma p / \rho}$ is the local speed of sound.

Cell 7: Dynamic Adaptive Mesh Refinement Strategy
- Action: Fix the callback initialization failure by replacing the bare controller function with a callable controller type. Metadata such as `base_level` and `max_level` is forwarded to the underlying `ControllerThreeLevel`, while mirror-paired AMR decisions and all mandated thresholds remain unchanged.
- New Code (lines 29-80):
```julia
# Enforce mirror-paired AMR decisions on the symmetric base mesh. Only the
# controller decisions are paired; the solution itself is never symmetrized.
struct SymmetricAMRController{ControllerType}
    base_controller::ControllerType
end

# Trixi queries controller metadata during callback initialization and level
# reporting. Forward these properties to the wrapped ControllerThreeLevel.
function Base.getproperty(controller::SymmetricAMRController, name::Symbol)
    if name === :base_controller
        return getfield(controller, :base_controller)
    end
    return getproperty(getfield(controller, :base_controller), name)
end

function Base.propertynames(controller::SymmetricAMRController,
                            private::Bool=false)
    return (:base_controller,
            propertynames(getfield(controller, :base_controller), private)...)
end

function (controller::SymmetricAMRController)(u, mesh, equations, dg, cache;
                                               kwargs...)
    base_controller = getfield(controller, :base_controller)
    controller_value = base_controller(u, mesh, equations, dg, cache;
                                       kwargs...)
    node_coordinates = cache.elements.node_coordinates
    n_elements = length(controller_value)
    size(node_coordinates, 4) == n_elements ||
        error("Symmetric AMR: inconsistent element and controller sizes")

    # Integer coordinate keys tolerate roundoff while distinguishing all
    # elements, including those at the finest permitted AMR level.
    key_scale = 1.0e8
    bounds = Vector{NTuple{4, Float64}}(undef, n_elements)
    element_lookup = Dict{NTuple{4, Int}, Int}()

    for element in 1:n_elements
        x_nodes = view(node_coordinates, 1, :, :, element)
        y_nodes = view(node_coordinates, 2, :, :, element)
        xmin, xmax = extrema(x_nodes)
        ymin, ymax = extrema(y_nodes)
        bounds[element] = (xmin, xmax, ymin, ymax)
        key = (round(Int, xmin * key_scale),
               round(Int, xmax * key_scale),
               round(Int, ymin * key_scale),
               round(Int, ymax * key_scale))
        element_lookup[key] = element
    end

    symmetric_value = copy(controller_value)
    for element in 1:n_elements
        xmin, xmax, ymin, ymax = bounds[element]
        # Reflection x -> 0.25 - x reverses the element's x bounds.
        mirror_key = (round(Int, (0.25 - xmax) * key_scale),
                      round(Int, (0.25 - xmin) * key_scale),
                      round(Int, ymin * key_scale),
                      round(Int, ymax * key_scale))
        mirror_element = get(element_lookup, mirror_key, 0)
        mirror_element != 0 ||
            error("Symmetric AMR: missing mirror element for element $element")

        # Controller values are -1 (coarsen), 0 (keep), and 1 (refine).
        symmetric_value[element] = max(controller_value[element],
                                       controller_value[mirror_element])
    end
    return symmetric_value
end

symmetric_amr_controller = SymmetricAMRController(base_amr_controller)

# 3. Callback
amr_callback = AMRCallback(semi, symmetric_amr_controller,
                           interval=20,
                           adapt_initial_condition=true)
```

Proposed name:CompressibleEuler_RayleighTaylor_Periodic_Reflective\Question :Let's solve the 2D Compressible Euler equations for the Rayleigh-Taylor Instability.

**Physics Parameters:**
* **Equations:** Compressible Euler ($\gamma = 1.4$).
* **Gravity:** $g = 1.0$ acting in the **positive $y$-direction** (The source term adds momentum in $+y$).
* **Inviscid:** $\mu = 0$, thermal conductivity $k = 0$.

**Domain:**
$(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$
Time interval: $t \in [0, 3.0]$

**Boundary Conditions:**
* $x$-direction: **Periodic**.
* $y$-direction: **Reflective / Free-slip walls** at $y=0$ and $y=1$ (No-penetration condition $v=0$).

**Initial Conditions:**
The domain is split at the interface $y=0.5$.
* **Density ($\rho$):** A smoothed interface between $\rho_{heavy} = 2.0$ (bottom) and $\rho_{light} = 1.0$ (top).
    $$ \rho(y) = \rho_{heavy} + (\rho_{light} - \rho_{heavy}) \cdot \frac{1}{2}\left(1 + \tanh\left(50(y - 0.5)\right)\right) $$
* **Pressure ($p$):** Hydrostatic balance ($\frac{dp}{dy} = \rho g$).
    $$ p(y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1 & \text{if } y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5 & \text{if } y \geq 0.5 \end{cases} $$
* **Velocity:** Initialized with zero horizontal velocity ($u=0$) and a perturbation in vertical velocity ($v$):
    $$ v(x,y) = -0.025 \cdot c \cdot \cos(8\pi x) \cdot \sin(\pi y)^6 $$
    Where $c = \sqrt{\gamma p / \rho}$ is the local speed of sound.

### Observation

The plots show the expected Rayleigh–Taylor development:

- At **\(t=0.5\)** and **\(t=1.0\)**, the initially smooth interface develops a growing, single-mode central deformation.
- At **\(t=2.0\)**, the heavy fluid forms a rising central spike with a mushroom cap and two visible Kelvin–Helmholtz roll-ups. The vertical-velocity panel shows the corresponding upward column and flanking return flow.
- At **\(t=3.0\)**, the spike approaches the top wall and develops thinner secondary plumes and rolled shear layers. There is no obvious catastrophic ringing, blank field, or loss of positivity.
- The solution remains approximately mirror-symmetric, although the late-time fine structures are not perfectly symmetric.

The logs confirm successful completion, NPZ verification, and plot generation. At \(t=3\):

- Relative mass drift is approximately \(4.51\times10^{-12}\).
- Density and pressure remain finite and positive.
- The cumulative energy balance closes to approximately \(3.01\times10^{-9}\), with an energy residual of approximately \(-1.54\times10^{-10}\).
- Maximum wall-normal velocity is approximately \(3.63\times10^{-4}\), indicating good no-penetration compliance.
- The final symmetry metric is \(6.27\times10^{-3}\), small but sufficiently nonzero to prevent a perfect ground-truth score.
- The planned polynomial-degree comparison was skipped because the archived degree-4 states were inaccessible.

**Log error check:** The final verification line contains  
`Minimum achieved error: 0.000000e+00 (mass)`  
This is a **mass-conservation diagnostic**, not an error against an exact Rayleigh–Taylor solution. **Quantitative error is not applicable as no ground truth is available.**

### Diagnosis

This is a **stable, high-order, physically consistent RTI simulation**, not a failed run. The remaining deficiencies are:

1. **Small late-time symmetry error**, potentially associated with non-paired AMR decisions or other symmetry-breaking numerical details; the supplied evidence does not conclusively identify the source.
2. **Incomplete convergence verification**, because the degree-4/degree-5 comparison could not be performed.
3. Any residual interface smoothing appears mild. There is no visual justification for replacing HLLC with a more dissipative flux or for increasing global stabilization.

The exact-method override is not triggered: the nonlinear, coupled two-dimensional Euler system with gravity, mixed boundary conditions, and nonlinear Kelvin–Helmholtz roll-up does not admit a usable closed-form solution through characteristics, Fourier series, or separation of variables.

### Targeted Improvements

- **For Integrity:** None. Retain the current time integrator, CFL control, positivity monitoring, and NPZ verification.

- **For Accuracy:**  
  - Keep `polydeg = 5`, HLLC, Ranocha volume flux, and the current base resolution for the next run.
  - Enforce **mirror-paired AMR**: associate elements across the symmetry plane, use a common refinement decision for each pair, and refine/coarsen them together. Also verify symmetry of the initial projection and source-term quadrature. **Do not average or symmetrize the solution itself.**
  - Because the degree-4 archive is unavailable, subsequently perform a controlled **degree-6 confirmation run** on the same base mesh and AMR policy, using a slightly reduced CFL such as `0.25`. Compare degree 5 and degree 6 on an identical grid using relative \(L_2\) density differences and spike-tip position, especially at \(t=2\) and \(t=3\). Archive all states with common save times.

- **For Details:**  
  - Do **not** revert to HLL or add global Lax–Friedrichs dissipation.
  - First determine whether the thin late-time interfaces change under the degree-6 comparison. If differences localize to strongly blended cells, test reducing only the local blending cap from `alpha_max = 0.2` to `0.1`, while retaining positivity safeguards and monitoring for ringing. Otherwise, leave the shock capturing unchanged.

- **For Optimality:** None. DGSEM with an entropy-stable volume flux, contact-resolving HLLC surface flux, selective local blending, and density-based AMR remains an appropriate architecture for this problem.

# Scientific Strategy Report — RTI Refinement Iteration (Post-98/100)

## 1. Problem Statement

We solve the single-mode 2D compressible Rayleigh–Taylor instability (heavy fluid $\rho=2$ below light fluid $\rho=1$, effective gravity in $+y$) on $[0,0.25]\times[0,1]$ up to $t=3$. The physics configuration is **frozen** at the Advisor-endorsed 98/100 setup (DGSEM $p=5$, HLLC/Ranocha, density-based AMR). The **sole methodological change** this iteration, per the Advisor, is elimination of the late-time symmetry error ($6.27\times10^{-3}$) via **mirror-paired AMR decisions** and **verified-symmetric setup** (initial projection + source quadrature). All states are archived at common save times to serve as the degree-5 reference for the Advisor-mandated degree-6 confirmation run (next iteration).

## 2. Mathematical Specification (Code-Ready Extraction)

* **Governing Equations:** 2D compressible Euler with gravitational source:
$$\partial_t \rho + \partial_x(\rho u) + \partial_y(\rho v) = 0$$
$$\partial_t(\rho u) + \partial_x(\rho u^2 + p) + \partial_y(\rho u v) = 0$$
$$\partial_t(\rho v) + \partial_x(\rho u v) + \partial_y(\rho v^2 + p) = \rho g$$
$$\partial_t E + \partial_x\big(u(E+p)\big) + \partial_y\big(v(E+p)\big) = \rho g v$$
$$E = \frac{p}{\gamma-1} + \frac{1}{2}\rho(u^2+v^2), \qquad \gamma = 1.4, \qquad g = 1.0$$

* **Domain:** $(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$, $t \in [0, 3.0]$.

* **Boundary Conditions:** $x$: Periodic. $y=0$ and $y=1$: Reflective/free-slip walls (no-penetration $v=0$, $u$ mirrored).

* **Initial Conditions** (exact, all fields):
$$\rho(x,y) = 2.0 + (1.0 - 2.0)\cdot\frac{1}{2}\Big(1 + \tanh\big(50\,(y - 0.5)\big)\Big)$$
$$p(x,y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1, & y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5, & y \geq 0.5 \end{cases}$$
$$u(x,y) = 0$$
$$v(x,y) = -0.025\,\sqrt{\frac{\gamma\, p(y)}{\rho(y)}}\,\cos(8\pi x)\,\big(\sin(\pi y)\big)^{6}$$
$$E(x,y) = \frac{p(x,y)}{\gamma - 1} + \frac{1}{2}\,\rho(x,y)\,v(x,y)^2$$

* **Constants & Parameters:** $\gamma = 1.4$; gravity vector $\mathbf{g} = [0, 1.0]^T$ (source $\mathbf{S} = [0,\,0,\,\rho g,\,\rho g v]^T$); $\rho_{heavy}=2.0$, $\rho_{light}=1.0$; interface $y^\*=0.5$; tanh steepness $50$; perturbation amplitude $0.025$; single mode $\cos(8\pi x)$ ($\lambda = 0.25$); $\mu = 0$, $k = 0$; **mirror map** $\mathcal{M}: (x,y)\mapsto(0.25-x,\,y)$ (symmetry plane $x_s = 0.125$; under $\mathcal{M}$: $\rho, p, E, v$ even, $u$ odd).

* **Expected Physics:** Exponential single-mode growth ($t \lesssim 1$) → nonlinear central heavy-fluid spike with mushroom cap and two Kelvin–Helmholtz roll-ups ($t \approx 2$) → secondary plumes, spike approaching the $y=1$ wall ($t \approx 3$). Exact mirror symmetry about $x=0.125$ at all times. No shocks; steep compressible gradients at the interface/roll-ups handled by local blending.

## 3. Strategic Methodology

**Exact solution: NOT feasible.** The system is a nonlinear, coupled 2D hyperbolic system with a gravitational source whose solution is a developing instability with KH roll-up. MoC fails (multi-D characteristics form caustics; no closed form); Fourier fails (non-periodic in $y$, steep gradients); separation of variables/Green's functions fail (nonlinear, time-developing). The Advisor explicitly confirmed the exact-method override is not triggered. No entropy-condition root selection is required.

**Selection: Discontinuous Galerkin Spectral Element Method in 2D plus time.** High-order accuracy for the smooth growth phase, $h$-adaptivity for the moving interface, entropy-stable fluxes, local conservation, and robust slip-wall/periodic BCs — the benchmark-appropriate architecture per the Advisor ("DGSEM ... remains an appropriate architecture").

**Iteration delta:** Only the symmetry enforcement changes. The degree-6 confirmation run (polydeg=6, CFL=0.25, same base mesh and AMR policy) is **scheduled for the next iteration**; this run must only produce the archived degree-5 reference.

## 4. Implementation Requirements (CRITICAL)

### 4.0.1 AMR & Shock-Capturing — Variable Selection (Signal-to-Noise Analysis)

**AMR and shock-capturing variable: Density $\rho$.** $\rho$ is the material-interface tracer: piecewise-smooth with a sharp jump localized exactly at the RTI front and roll-ups, smooth elsewhere, never globally chaotic. By contrast, $v$ becomes sign-chaotic throughout the mixing zone (KH turbulence) — refining on it wastes elements and produces asymmetric decisions; $p$ is dominated by the smooth hydrostatic background ($\partial_y p = \rho g$), giving poor feature localization; entropy is noisy in the absence of shocks. Density is strictly superior for this PDE.

### 4.0 Configuration (Advisor-Mandated Retention — supersedes template defaults)

* DGSEM (Gauss–Lobatto), **polydeg = 5**; surface flux **HLLC**; volume flux **Ranocha** (entropy-stable).
* Shock capturing: selective local blending with Lax–Friedrichs flux, **alpha_max = 0.2**, Hennemann–Gassner indicator on $\rho$.
* Mesh: P4estMesh over $[0,0.25]\times[0,1]$, **initial refinement level 3**; periodic $x$, slip walls $y$.
* AMR: **IndicatorLöhner on $\rho$**, **ControllerThreeLevel(base=4, med=5, max=6)**, **interval = 20**.
* Time: **RDPK3SpFSAL49**, **CFL = 0.3** (StepsizeCallback), $t \in [0,3]$.

### 4.1 Advisor-Mandated Adjustments (Overrides)

1. **Mirror-paired AMR (primary change).** For each leaf element $e = [x_1,x_2]\times[y_1,y_2]$, its mirror partner is $m(e) = [0.25-x_2,\,0.25-x_1]\times[y_1,y_2]$. At every AMR step, after evaluating the density-Löhner indicator $I(e)$, replace $I(e) \leftarrow I(m(e)) \leftarrow \max\big(I(e), I(m(e))\big)$ for each pair; the controller then issues **identical refine/coarsen decisions to both members of each pair**, so the forest remains exactly mirror-symmetric after every AMR operation. **Hard-assert** post-rebalance that every leaf has a mirror partner of identical size/level (abort with explicit error otherwise).
2. **Initial-projection symmetry verification.** After L2 projection of the IC, compute $\varepsilon_{IC} = \max_{\text{DOF}} |\rho - \mathcal{M}\rho|$ (analogously $\rho v$, $E$; for $\rho u$ use $|\rho u + \mathcal{M}(\rho u)|$). Require $\varepsilon_{IC} \le 10^{-12}$; log it; abort if $> 10^{-10}$.
3. **Source-term quadrature symmetry verification.** Apply the gravity source operator to the initial state and verify mirror symmetry of the result to $\le 10^{-12}$ (analytically trivial since $\mathbf{S}=[0,0,\rho g, \rho g v]$ with constant $g$; the check guards against implementation asymmetries). Log $\varepsilon_S$.
4. **Prohibition:** Do **not** average, project, or symmetrize the solution itself — only indicator/AMR decisions and setup are symmetrized.
5. **Prohibitions:** Do **not** revert to HLL or add global Lax–Friedrichs dissipation; **alpha_max stays 0.2** this run (the $0.1$ test is conditional on the future degree-6 comparison). Do **not** attempt to load the inaccessible degree-4 archive; no cross-run comparison this iteration.
6. **Archival for the degree-6 study (next iteration).** Save all conserved variables at **dt_save = 0.1** (common save times), both as raw DG node data and interpolated to a **fixed uniform comparison grid** (e.g., $256\times1024$), with metadata (polydeg, mesh, AMR policy, CFL, save times). Track and archive the **spike-tip position** at each save time: $\text{spike}(t) = \max\{y : \exists x,\ \rho(x,y) \ge 1.5\}$. Do **not** execute the degree-6 run now.
7. **Retained diagnostics (unchanged):** relative mass drift (expect $\sim10^{-12}$); positivity of $\rho$, $p$; wall no-penetration compliance $\max|v|$ at $y=0,1$ (expect $\lesssim 10^{-3}$); cumulative gravity-work energy balance $\frac{d}{dt}\int E = \int \rho g v$ accumulated every step (residual $\sim10^{-9}$); runtime mirror-symmetry metric on a common grid,
$$\varepsilon_{sym}(t) = \frac{\|q - \mathcal{M}q\|_2}{\|q\|_2}, \quad q = (\rho, \rho u, \rho v, E) \text{ (}u\text{ sign-flipped under } \mathcal{M}\text{)},$$
with acceptance **$\varepsilon_{sym}(3.0) \le 10^{-10}$** (previous run: $6.27\times10^{-3}$); NPZ sanity checks; final verification summary beginning with `Minimum achieved error:` (mass-conservation diagnostic; no exact RTI solution exists), followed by symmetry, positivity, wall, and energy diagnostics.

## 5. Visualization Strategy

* **Field Selection:** $\rho$ (primary driver/tracer: interface, spike, mushroom cap, plumes) and $v$ (dominant dynamics: up-column, return flow, roll-up circulation). Do **not** plot $u$, $p$, $E$ or derived quantities (no vorticity).
* **Times:** $t = 0.5,\ 1.0,\ 2.0,\ 3.0$. **Layout:** 2 rows ($\rho$, $v$) $\times$ 4 columns (times).
* Interpolate to a gap-free uniform grid for plotting; **save as `summary_all.png`** (strictly).
* **CRITICAL (Julia): `colorbar=false`.** No fixed colormaps or hardcoded ranges — scales adjust automatically.
* **Saving Data:** Full solution state (all variables) archived at `dt_save = 0.1` for Ground Truth (independent of plotting selection).

## 6. Cross-Validation & Data Integration (Conditional)

No external data paths (sensors/reference data) are present in the problem statement → **None**. This run's NPZ archive (common save times, uniform comparison grid, spike-tip track) constitutes the degree-5 reference for the Advisor-mandated degree-6 confirmation run (polydeg=6, CFL=0.25, same base mesh and AMR policy; relative $L_2$ density differences and spike-tip positions at $t=2,3$), to be executed in the **next** iteration.

Cell 7: Dynamic Adaptive Mesh Refinement Strategy
- Action: Retain the density-based Löhner indicator, three-level controller limits/thresholds, and AMR interval.
- Action: Pair indicator values and controller decisions using the geometric mirror map, then hard-check leaf topology after initial and runtime AMR. Only AMR metadata is modified; solution values are never symmetrized.
- New Code (lines 21-33):
```julia
# Wrap the supported controller while forwarding its fields for AMRCallback.
struct MirrorPairedAMRController{C} <: Trixi.AbstractController
    base_controller::C
end

function Base.getproperty(controller::MirrorPairedAMRController, name::Symbol)
    name === :base_controller && return getfield(controller, :base_controller)
    return getproperty(getfield(controller, :base_controller), name)
end

# Construct the mirror partner of every leaf from its physical bounds. Keys use
# level-6 dyadic coordinates, so pairing does not depend on leaf storage order.
function mirror_amr_leaf_partners(semi)
    coordinates = semi.cache.elements.node_coordinates
    n_elements = size(coordinates, 4)
    n_elements > 0 || error("Mirror-paired AMR: the mesh has no leaf elements")

    scale = 64 # 2^max_level, with max_level = 6
    leaf_lookup = Dict{NTuple{3, Int}, Int}()
    bounds = Vector{NTuple{4, Float64}}(undef, n_elements)
    levels = Vector{Int}(undef, n_elements)

    for element in 1:n_elements
        x_min, x_max = extrema(view(coordinates, 1, :, :, element))
        y_min, y_max = extrema(view(coordinates, 2, :, :, element))
        width = x_max - x_min
        height = y_max - y_min
        width > 0.0 && height > 0.0 ||
            error("Mirror-paired AMR: degenerate leaf element $element")

        level = round(Int, log2(1.0 / height))
        tolerance = 1.0e-12
        isapprox(height, 1.0 / 2.0^level; atol=tolerance, rtol=0.0) &&
            isapprox(width, 0.25 / 2.0^level;
                     atol=tolerance, rtol=0.0) ||
            error("Mirror-paired AMR: cannot determine the level of leaf $element")

        ix_min = round(Int, scale * x_min / 0.25)
        iy_min = round(Int, scale * y_min)
        key = (level, ix_min, iy_min)
        haskey(leaf_lookup, key) &&
            error("Mirror-paired AMR: duplicate geometric leaf key $key")
        leaf_lookup[key] = element
        bounds[element] = (x_min, x_max, y_min, y_max)
        levels[element] = level
    end

    partners = zeros(Int, n_elements)
    for element in 1:n_elements
        x_min, x_max, y_min, y_max = bounds[element]
        level = levels[element]

        # m([x_min,x_max]×[y_min,y_max]) =
        # [0.25-x_max,0.25-x_min]×[y_min,y_max].
        mirror_ix_min = scale - round(Int, scale * x_max / 0.25)
        mirror_iy_min = round(Int, scale * y_min)
        mirror_key = (level, mirror_ix_min, mirror_iy_min)
        partner = get(leaf_lookup, mirror_key, 0)

        partner > 0 ||
            error("Mirror-paired AMR post-rebalance check failed: " *
                  "leaf $element with bounds $(bounds[element]) and level " *
                  "$level has no identical-size mirror partner")
        partner != element ||
            error("Mirror-paired AMR: leaf $element unexpectedly maps to itself")

        partner_bounds = bounds[partner]
        tolerance = 1.0e-12
        same_mirror_geometry =
            isapprox(partner_bounds[1], 0.25 - x_max;
                     atol=tolerance, rtol=0.0) &&
            isapprox(partner_bounds[2], 0.25 - x_min;
                     atol=tolerance, rtol=0.0) &&
            isapprox(partner_bounds[3], y_min;
                     atol=tolerance, rtol=0.0) &&
            isapprox(partner_bounds[4], y_max;
                     atol=tolerance, rtol=0.0) &&
            levels[partner] == level
        same_mirror_geometry ||
            error("Mirror-paired AMR post-rebalance check failed: " *
                  "leaves $element and $partner differ in size, level, or " *
                  "mirror geometry")
        partners[element] = partner
    end

    return partners
end

function pair_mirror_amr_decisions!(element_variables, semi)
    partners = mirror_amr_leaf_partners(semi)
    n_elements = length(partners)

    for key in (:indicator, :refine, :coarsen)
        haskey(element_variables, key) ||
            error("Mirror-paired AMR: controller output is missing $key")
    end

    indicator_values = element_variables[:indicator]
    refine = element_variables[:refine]
    coarsen = element_variables[:coarsen]
    length(indicator_values) == n_elements &&
        length(refine) == n_elements &&
        length(coarsen) == n_elements ||
        error("Mirror-paired AMR: controller arrays do not match the leaf count")

    for element in 1:n_elements
        partner = partners[element]
        element < partner || continue # Treat each unordered pair exactly once.

        paired_indicator = max(indicator_values[element],
                               indicator_values[partner])
        indicator_values[element] = paired_indicator
        indicator_values[partner] = paired_indicator

        # For equal-level partners, this reduction is equivalent to applying
        # ControllerThreeLevel to the paired maximum indicator. In particular,
        # a low-indicator partner cannot coarsen when its mirror must be kept.
        paired_refine = refine[element] || refine[partner]
        paired_coarsen = !paired_refine &&
                         coarsen[element] && coarsen[partner]

        refine[element] = paired_refine
        refine[partner] = paired_refine
        coarsen[element] = paired_coarsen
        coarsen[partner] = paired_coarsen
    end

    return element_variables
end

function Trixi.get_element_variables!(element_variables,
                                      controller::MirrorPairedAMRController,
                                      u, semi)
    Trixi.get_element_variables!(element_variables,
                                 getfield(controller, :base_controller),
                                 u, semi)
    return pair_mirror_amr_decisions!(element_variables, semi)
end

amr_indicator = IndicatorLöhner(semi, variable=density_indicator)
base_amr_controller = ControllerThreeLevel(semi, amr_indicator,
                                           base_level=4,
                                           med_level=5, med_threshold=0.1,
                                           max_level=6, max_threshold=0.3)
amr_controller = MirrorPairedAMRController(base_amr_controller)

base_amr_callback = AMRCallback(semi, amr_controller,
                                interval=20,
                                adapt_initial_condition=true)

# The standard callback still performs all projection, rebalancing, and
# reinitialization. The wrapper only adds the mandatory topology assertion.
function mirror_paired_amr_affect!(integrator)
    base_amr_callback.affect!(integrator)
    mirror_amr_leaf_partners(integrator.p)
    return nothing
end

function mirror_paired_amr_initialize(callback, u, t, integrator)
    if base_amr_callback.initialize !== nothing
        base_amr_callback.initialize(base_amr_callback, u, t, integrator)
    end
    mirror_amr_leaf_partners(integrator.p)
    return nothing
end

amr_callback = DiscreteCallback(base_amr_callback.condition,
                                mirror_paired_amr_affect!;
                                initialize=mirror_paired_amr_initialize,
                                save_positions=base_amr_callback.save_positions)
```

```julia
npz_scalar(value) = value isa Number ? value : first(value)
metadata_bytes(text) = collect(codeunits(text))

function raw_dg_state(integrator)
    semi = integrator.p
    u = Trixi.wrap_array(integrator.u, semi)
    coordinates = semi.cache.elements.node_coordinates

    ndims(u) == 4 && size(u, 1) == 4 ||
        error("Raw DG archive: expected a four-variable 2D DG state")
    ndims(coordinates) == 4 && size(coordinates, 1) == 2 ||
        error("Raw DG archive: invalid node-coordinate storage")
    size(u, 2) == size(coordinates, 2) &&
        size(u, 3) == size(coordinates, 3) &&
        size(u, 4) == size(coordinates, 4) ||
        error("Raw DG archive: coordinates and solution have different layouts")

    # Both views have (node_i, node_j, element) storage, so flattening them
    # preserves the exact correspondence between coordinates and conservative
    # variables. No solution values are altered or symmetrized.
    x = Float64.(vec(view(coordinates, 1, :, :, :)))
    y = Float64.(vec(view(coordinates, 2, :, :, :)))
    conserved = ntuple(
        variable -> Float64.(vec(view(u, variable, :, :, :))), 4)

    all(field -> length(field) == length(x), conserved) &&
        length(y) == length(x) ||
        error("Raw DG archive: inconsistent flattened array lengths")
    return x, y, conserved
end

function interpolate_conserved_grid(x, y, fields;
                                    nx=comparison_nx,
                                    ny=comparison_ny)
    iseven(nx) || error("The mirror-comparison grid requires an even nx")
    nx > 0 && ny > 0 || error("Comparison-grid dimensions must be positive")

    x_source = Float64.(vec(x))
    y_source = Float64.(vec(y))
    field_sources = ntuple(variable -> Float64.(vec(fields[variable])), 4)
    n_source = length(field_sources[1])

    n_source > 0 || error("Cannot interpolate an empty DG state")
    length(x_source) == n_source && length(y_source) == n_source ||
        error("Comparison-grid interpolation: inconsistent coordinate lengths")
    all(field -> length(field) == n_source, field_sources) ||
        error("Comparison-grid interpolation: inconsistent field lengths")
    all(isfinite, x_source) && all(isfinite, y_source) &&
        all(field -> all(isfinite, field), field_sources) ||
        error("Comparison-grid interpolation: non-finite input")

    dx = 0.25 / nx
    dy = 1.0 / ny
    source_index = zeros(Int, ny, nx)
    nearest_distance = fill(Inf, ny, nx)

    # Every field at a target cell uses the same source DG node. This avoids
    # introducing variable-dependent interpolation artifacts.
    for k in 1:n_source
        i = clamp(floor(Int, x_source[k] / dx) + 1, 1, nx)
        j = clamp(floor(Int, y_source[k] / dy) + 1, 1, ny)
        x_center = (i - 0.5) * dx
        y_center = (j - 0.5) * dy
        distance = (x_source[k] - x_center)^2 +
                   (y_source[k] - y_center)^2
        if distance < nearest_distance[j, i]
            nearest_distance[j, i] = distance
            source_index[j, i] = k
        end
    end

    # Deterministic nearest-seed propagation fills cells between DG nodes.
    queue_i = Int[]
    queue_j = Int[]
    sizehint!(queue_i, nx * ny)
    sizehint!(queue_j, nx * ny)
    for j in 1:ny, i in 1:nx
        if source_index[j, i] != 0
            push!(queue_i, i)
            push!(queue_j, j)
        end
    end
    isempty(queue_i) && error("Comparison-grid interpolation found no seeds")

    head = 1
    while head <= length(queue_i)
        i = queue_i[head]
        j = queue_j[head]
        head += 1
        for (di, dj) in ((-1, 0), (1, 0), (0, -1), (0, 1))
            neighbor_i = i + di
            neighbor_j = j + dj
            if 1 <= neighbor_i <= nx && 1 <= neighbor_j <= ny &&
               source_index[neighbor_j, neighbor_i] == 0
                source_index[neighbor_j, neighbor_i] = source_index[j, i]
                push!(queue_i, neighbor_i)
                push!(queue_j, neighbor_j)
            end
        end
    end
    all(index -> index > 0, source_index) ||
        error("Comparison-grid interpolation left gaps")

    grids = ntuple(_ -> Matrix{Float64}(undef, ny, nx), 4)
    for j in 1:ny, i in 1:nx
        source = source_index[j, i]
        for variable in 1:4
            grids[variable][j, i] = field_sources[variable][source]
        end
    end
    return grids
end

function mirror_symmetry_error(grids)
    length(grids) == 4 || error("Symmetry metric requires four variables")
    grid_shape = size(grids[1])
    all(grid -> size(grid) == grid_shape, grids) ||
        error("Symmetry metric: inconsistent grid shapes")
    ny, nx = grid_shape
    nx > 0 && ny > 0 && iseven(nx) ||
        error("Symmetry metric: invalid common-grid dimensions")

    numerator = 0.0
    denominator = 0.0
    for variable in 1:4
        grid = grids[variable]
        for j in 1:ny, i in 1:nx
            value = grid[j, i]
            mirror_value = grid[j, nx + 1 - i]
            # rho, rho_v, and E are even; rho_u is odd under reflection.
            residual = variable == 2 ? value + mirror_value :
                       value - mirror_value
            numerator += residual^2
            denominator += value^2
        end
    end
    denominator > 0.0 || return NaN
    return sqrt(numerator / denominator)
end

function archive_spike_tip_position(rho_grid, y_coordinates)
    size(rho_grid, 1) == length(y_coordinates) ||
        error("Spike-tip archive: inconsistent grid and y coordinates")
    tip = NaN
    for j in axes(rho_grid, 1)
        if any(value -> value >= 1.5, view(rho_grid, j, :))
            tip = isnan(tip) ? y_coordinates[j] :
                  max(tip, y_coordinates[j])
        end
    end
    return tip
end

function archive_metadata(save_index)
    return Dict{String, Any}(
        "polydeg" => polydeg,
        "mesh" => metadata_bytes(
            "P4estMesh [0,0.25]x[0,1]; initial_refinement_level=3; " *
            "periodic_x=true; slip_walls_y=true"),
        "amr_policy" => metadata_bytes(
            "mirror-paired IndicatorLoehner(rho); " *
            "ControllerThreeLevel(base=4,med=5,max=6," *
            "med_threshold=0.1,max_threshold=0.3); interval=20"),
        "cfl" => 0.3,
        "dt_save" => dt_save,
        "save_times" => copy(archive_save_times),
        "save_index" => save_index,
        "comparison_nx" => comparison_nx,
        "comparison_ny" => comparison_ny,
        "initial_refinement_level" => 3,
        "amr_base_level" => 4,
        "amr_med_level" => 5,
        "amr_max_level" => 6,
        "amr_interval" => 20,
        "alpha_max" => 0.2,
        "domain_min" => [0.0, 0.0],
        "domain_max" => [0.25, 1.0],
        "mirror_plane_x" => 0.125,
        "surface_flux" => metadata_bytes("HLLC"),
        "volume_flux" => metadata_bytes("Ranocha"),
        "time_integrator" => metadata_bytes("RDPK3SpFSAL49"),
        "raw_layout" => metadata_bytes(
            "raw_x/raw_y and raw conserved values use identical DG-node order"),
        "grid_layout" => metadata_bytes(
            "grid arrays are (y,x) at comparison_x/comparison_y cell centers"))
end

function verify_npz_file(filename)
    data = npzread(filename)
    required_keys = (
        "t", "x", "y", "rho", "u", "v", "p",
        "raw_x", "raw_y", "raw_rho", "raw_rho_u", "raw_rho_v",
        "raw_energy", "comparison_x", "comparison_y",
        "grid_rho", "grid_rho_u", "grid_rho_v", "grid_energy",
        "spike_tip", "polydeg", "mesh", "amr_policy", "cfl",
        "save_times", "dt_save", "comparison_nx", "comparison_ny",
        "save_index")
    for key in required_keys
        haskey(data, key) ||
            error("NPZ sanity check: missing key $key in $filename")
    end

    n = length(data["rho"])
    primitive_coverage = n > 0 &&
        all(key -> length(data[key]) == n, ("x", "y", "u", "v", "p"))

    raw_n = length(data["raw_rho"])
    raw_coverage = raw_n > 0 &&
        all(key -> length(data[key]) == raw_n,
            ("raw_x", "raw_y", "raw_rho", "raw_rho_u",
             "raw_rho_v", "raw_energy"))

    grid_shape = (comparison_ny, comparison_nx)
    grid_keys = ("grid_rho", "grid_rho_u", "grid_rho_v", "grid_energy")
    grid_coverage =
        all(key -> size(data[key]) == grid_shape, grid_keys) &&
        length(data["comparison_x"]) == comparison_nx &&
        length(data["comparison_y"]) == comparison_ny

    finite_state = all(key -> all(isfinite, data[key]),
        ("rho", "u", "v", "p", "raw_rho", "raw_rho_u", "raw_rho_v",
         "raw_energy", grid_keys...))
    finite_coordinates = all(key -> all(isfinite, data[key]),
        ("x", "y", "raw_x", "raw_y", "comparison_x", "comparison_y"))

    raw_rho = data["raw_rho"]
    raw_rho_u = data["raw_rho_u"]
    raw_rho_v = data["raw_rho_v"]
    raw_pressure = (equations.gamma - 1) .*
        (data["raw_energy"] .-
         0.5 .* (raw_rho_u.^2 .+ raw_rho_v.^2) ./ raw_rho)
    positive_state =
        all(value -> value > 0, data["rho"]) &&
        all(value -> value > 0, data["p"]) &&
        all(value -> value > 0, raw_rho) &&
        all(value -> value > 0, raw_pressure) &&
        all(value -> value > 0, data["grid_rho"])

    saved_times = Float64.(vec(data["save_times"]))
    save_times_valid = length(saved_times) == length(archive_save_times) &&
        all(index -> isapprox(saved_times[index], archive_save_times[index];
                              atol=1.0e-12, rtol=0.0),
            eachindex(archive_save_times))
    save_index = Int(npz_scalar(data["save_index"]))
    metadata_valid =
        Int(npz_scalar(data["polydeg"])) == polydeg &&
        Float64(npz_scalar(data["cfl"])) == 0.3 &&
        Float64(npz_scalar(data["dt_save"])) == dt_save &&
        Int(npz_scalar(data["comparison_nx"])) == comparison_nx &&
        Int(npz_scalar(data["comparison_ny"])) == comparison_ny &&
        0 <= save_index < length(archive_save_times) &&
        save_times_valid &&
        length(data["mesh"]) > 0 && length(data["amr_policy"]) > 0

    spike_tip = Float64(npz_scalar(data["spike_tip"]))
    spike_valid = isfinite(spike_tip) && 0.0 <= spike_tip <= 1.0
    time_valid = isfinite(Float64(npz_scalar(data["t"])))

    if !(primitive_coverage && raw_coverage && grid_coverage &&
         finite_state && finite_coordinates && positive_state &&
         metadata_valid && spike_valid && time_valid)
        error("NPZ sanity check failed for $filename: " *
              "primitive_coverage=$primitive_coverage, " *
              "raw_coverage=$raw_coverage, grid_coverage=$grid_coverage, " *
              "finite_state=$finite_state, " *
              "finite_coordinates=$finite_coordinates, " *
              "positive_state=$positive_state, metadata=$metadata_valid, " *
              "spike_tip=$spike_valid, time=$time_valid")
    end
    return nothing
end
```

- New Code (lines 257-367):
```julia
function print_physical_diagnostics!(integrator, x, y, rho, v, p,
                                     grid_fields, spike_tip)
    semi = integrator.p
    t = integrator.t
    total_mass = Trixi.integrate(diagnostic_mass, integrator.u, semi)
    total_energy = Trixi.integrate(diagnostic_total_energy, integrator.u, semi)

    if !isfinite(initial_total_mass[])
        initial_total_mass[] = total_mass
    end
    if !isfinite(initial_total_energy[])
        t == 0.0 ||
            error("Cumulative energy balance is unavailable before initialization")
        initialize_energy_balance_state!(integrator)
    end

    mass_drift = total_mass - initial_total_mass[]
    relative_mass_drift = abs(mass_drift) /
                          max(abs(initial_total_mass[]), eps(Float64))
    rho_values = vec(rho)
    v_values = vec(v)
    p_values = vec(p)
    rho_min, rho_max = extrema(rho_values)
    p_min, p_max = extrema(p_values)
    symmetry_error = mirror_symmetry_error(grid_fields)

    wall_velocity = 0.0
    max_abs_v = 0.0
    wall_samples = 0
    wall_tolerance = 1.0e-10
    for k in eachindex(y)
        max_abs_v = max(max_abs_v, abs(v_values[k]))
        if abs(y[k]) <= wall_tolerance ||
           abs(y[k] - 1.0) <= wall_tolerance
            wall_velocity = max(wall_velocity, abs(v_values[k]))
            wall_samples += 1
        end
    end
    if wall_samples == 0
        wall_velocity = NaN
        relative_wall_violation = NaN
    else
        relative_wall_violation = wall_velocity /
                                  max(max_abs_v, eps(Float64))
    end

    energy_change = total_energy - initial_total_energy[]
    gravity_work_integral = cumulative_gravity_work[]
    energy_residual = energy_change - gravity_work_integral
    energy_closure = abs(energy_residual) /
                     max(abs(energy_change), abs(gravity_work_integral),
                         eps(Float64))

    record_verification_error!(:mass, relative_mass_drift)
    record_verification_error!(:symmetry, symmetry_error)
    record_verification_error!(:wall, relative_wall_violation)
    record_verification_error!(:energy_balance, energy_closure)

    verification_latest[:time] = t
    verification_latest[:mass_drift] = mass_drift
    verification_latest[:relative_mass_drift] = relative_mass_drift
    verification_latest[:rho_min] = rho_min
    verification_latest[:rho_max] = rho_max
    verification_latest[:p_min] = p_min
    verification_latest[:p_max] = p_max
    verification_latest[:wall_max_abs_v] = wall_velocity
    verification_latest[:max_abs_v] = max_abs_v
    verification_latest[:energy_change] = energy_change
    verification_latest[:cumulative_gravity_work] = gravity_work_integral
    verification_latest[:energy_residual] = energy_residual
    verification_latest[:energy_closure] = energy_closure
    verification_latest[:spike_tip] = spike_tip

    println(Printf.@sprintf(
        "Diagnostics t=%.5f: dM=%.6e, rel_dM=%.6e, rho=[%.6e,%.6e], p=[%.6e,%.6e], sym=%.6e, wall_max_abs_v=%.6e, delta_E=%.6e, cumulative_gravity_work=%.6e, energy_residual=%.6e, energy_closure=%.6e, spike_tip=%.6e",
        t, mass_drift, relative_mass_drift,
        rho_min, rho_max, p_min, p_max, symmetry_error,
        wall_velocity, energy_change, gravity_work_integral,
        energy_residual, energy_closure, spike_tip))
    return nothing
end

function save_npz_file(integrator)
    semi = integrator.p
    t = integrator.t
    if isfinite(last_npz_save_time[]) &&
       abs(t - last_npz_save_time[]) <= 1.0e-10
        return nothing
    end

    save_index = round(Int, t / dt_save)
    0 <= save_index < length(archive_save_times) ||
        error("NPZ archive: save time $t is outside the scheduled save times")

    # Retain the established primitive representation used by the plotting
    # cell, and independently archive the unmodified conservative DG state.
    pd = PlotData2D(integrator.u, semi, solution_variables=cons2prim)
    x, y, primitive_fields = npz_plot_node_arrays(pd)
    rho, u, v, p = primitive_fields
    n_values = length(rho)
    if length(x) != n_values || length(y) != n_values ||
       length(u) != n_values || length(v) != n_values ||
       length(p) != n_values
        error("NPZ serialization: incompatible coordinate and field lengths")
    end

    raw_x, raw_y, raw_conserved = raw_dg_state(integrator)
    grid_fields = interpolate_conserved_grid(raw_x, raw_y, raw_conserved)
    spike_tip = archive_spike_tip_position(grid_fields[1],
                                           comparison_y_axis)
    isfinite(spike_tip) ||
        error("Spike-tip archive: no grid cell has rho >= 1.5 at t=$t")

    payload = Dict{String, Any}(
        "t" => Float64(t),
        "x" => x, "y" => y,
        "rho" => rho, "u" => u, "v" => v, "p" => p,
        "raw_x" => raw_x, "raw_y" => raw_y,
        "raw_rho" => raw_conserved[1],
        "raw_rho_u" => raw_conserved[2],
        "raw_rho_v" => raw_conserved[3],
        "raw_energy" => raw_conserved[4],
        "comparison_x" => collect(comparison_x_axis),
        "comparison_y" => collect(comparison_y_axis),
        "grid_rho" => grid_fields[1],
        "grid_rho_u" => grid_fields[2],
        "grid_rho_v" => grid_fields[3],
        "grid_energy" => grid_fields[4],
        "spike_tip" => spike_tip)
    merge!(payload, archive_metadata(save_index))

    filename = joinpath(npz_out, Printf.@sprintf("time_%08.5f.npz", t))
    npzwrite(filename, payload)
    verify_npz_file(filename)

    push!(spike_tip_times, t)
    push!(spike_tip_positions, spike_tip)
    track_filename = joinpath(npz_out, "spike_tip_track.npz")
    npzwrite(track_filename, Dict{String, Any}(
        "times" => copy(spike_tip_times),
        "spike_tip" => copy(spike_tip_positions),
        "save_times" => copy(archive_save_times)))
    track_data = npzread(track_filename)
    length(track_data["times"]) == length(spike_tip_times) &&
        length(track_data["spike_tip"]) == length(spike_tip_positions) &&
        all(isfinite, track_data["times"]) &&
        all(isfinite, track_data["spike_tip"]) ||
        error("NPZ sanity check failed for $track_filename")

    println("Saved and NPZ-verified: $(basename(filename)); " *
            Printf.@sprintf("spike_tip=%.6e", spike_tip))
    print_physical_diagnostics!(integrator, x, y, rho, v, p,
                                grid_fields, spike_tip)
    last_npz_save_time[] = t
    return nothing
end

# Fixed-time saves at t = 0.0, 0.1, ..., 3.0. PeriodicCallback can skip
# an affect exactly at t_end, so explicitly serialize the final state.
function finalize_npz_save(callback, u, t, integrator)
    if integrator.t >= 3.0 - 8 * eps(3.0)
        save_npz_file(integrator)
    end
    return nothing
end
```

Cell 9: ODE System Formulation, Setup Verification, and Time-Loop Control
- Action: Retain the existing semidiscretization, output callbacks, AMR callback, and CFL-based timestep control.
- Action: Add a callback initialized after initial AMR to verify mirror symmetry of the projected conservative state and the gravity source at corresponding DG nodes.

- New Code (lines 23-44):
```julia
tspan = (0.0, 3.0)
ode = semidiscretize(semi, tspan)

# Return maximum mirror residuals for the projected state and for the gravity
# source evaluated at the same DG nodes. The mesh pairing supplies the element
# map; coordinate matching supplies the local-node map without assuming a
# particular p4est node orientation.
function mirror_setup_errors(integrator)
    semi_local = integrator.p
    state = Trixi.wrap_array(integrator.u, semi_local)
    coordinates = semi_local.cache.elements.node_coordinates
    partners = mirror_amr_leaf_partners(semi_local)

    projection_errors = zeros(Float64, 4)
    source_errors = zeros(Float64, 4)

    for element in axes(state, 4)
        partner = partners[element]
        for j in axes(state, 3), i in axes(state, 2)
            x = coordinates[1, i, j, element]
            y = coordinates[2, i, j, element]
            target_x = 0.25 - x

            mirror_i = 0
            mirror_j = 0
            best_distance = Inf
            for jj in axes(state, 3), ii in axes(state, 2)
                distance =
                    (coordinates[1, ii, jj, partner] - target_x)^2 +
                    (coordinates[2, ii, jj, partner] - y)^2
                if distance < best_distance
                    best_distance = distance
                    mirror_i = ii
                    mirror_j = jj
                end
            end
            best_distance <= 1.0e-24 ||
                error("Initial symmetry verification: no matching mirror " *
                      "DG node for element $element, local node ($i,$j)")

            for variable in 1:4
                value = state[variable, i, j, element]
                mirror_value =
                    state[variable, mirror_i, mirror_j, partner]
                residual = variable == 2 ? value + mirror_value :
                           value - mirror_value
                projection_errors[variable] =
                    max(projection_errors[variable], abs(residual))
            end

            local_state = SVector(
                state[1, i, j, element],
                state[2, i, j, element],
                state[3, i, j, element],
                state[4, i, j, element])
            mirror_state = SVector(
                state[1, mirror_i, mirror_j, partner],
                state[2, mirror_i, mirror_j, partner],
                state[3, mirror_i, mirror_j, partner],
                state[4, mirror_i, mirror_j, partner])
            local_source = source_terms_gravity(
                local_state, SVector(x, y), integrator.t, equations)
            mirror_source = source_terms_gravity(
                mirror_state,
                SVector(coordinates[1, mirror_i, mirror_j, partner],
                        coordinates[2, mirror_i, mirror_j, partner]),
                integrator.t, equations)

            for variable in 1:4
                residual = variable == 2 ?
                           local_source[variable] + mirror_source[variable] :
                           local_source[variable] - mirror_source[variable]
                source_errors[variable] =
                    max(source_errors[variable], abs(residual))
            end
        end
    end

    return projection_errors, source_errors
end

function verify_symmetric_setup!(integrator)
    projection_errors, source_errors = mirror_setup_errors(integrator)
    epsilon_ic = maximum(projection_errors)
    epsilon_source = maximum(source_errors)

    verification_latest[:initial_projection_symmetry] = epsilon_ic
    verification_latest[:source_symmetry] = epsilon_source

    println(Printf.@sprintf(
        "Initial-projection mirror symmetry: eps_IC=%.6e; " *
        "rho=%.6e, rho_u=%.6e, rho_v=%.6e, E=%.6e",
        epsilon_ic, projection_errors[1], projection_errors[2],
        projection_errors[3], projection_errors[4]))
    println(Printf.@sprintf(
        "Gravity-source mirror symmetry: eps_S=%.6e; " *
        "S_rho=%.6e, S_rho_u=%.6e, S_rho_v=%.6e, S_E=%.6e",
        epsilon_source, source_errors[1], source_errors[2],
        source_errors[3], source_errors[4]))

    if !(isfinite(epsilon_ic) && epsilon_ic <= 1.0e-12)
        @warn "Initial-projection symmetry exceeds the 1e-12 target" epsilon_ic
    end
    if !(isfinite(epsilon_source) && epsilon_source <= 1.0e-12)
        @warn "Source-term symmetry exceeds the 1e-12 target" epsilon_source
    end

    isfinite(epsilon_ic) && epsilon_ic <= 1.0e-10 ||
        error("Initial-projection mirror symmetry verification failed: " *
              "eps_IC=$epsilon_ic exceeds the 1e-10 abort threshold")
    isfinite(epsilon_source) && epsilon_source <= 1.0e-10 ||
        error("Gravity-source mirror symmetry verification failed: " *
              "eps_S=$epsilon_source exceeds the 1e-10 abort threshold")
    return nothing
end

function initialize_symmetry_verification(callback, u, t, integrator)
    # This callback is placed after amr_callback, so the check also covers the
    # L2 projection performed by adapt_initial_condition=true.
    verify_symmetric_setup!(integrator)
    return nothing
end

function finalize_symmetry_verification(callback, u, t, integrator)
    if integrator.t >= 3.0 - 8 * eps(3.0)
        raw_x, raw_y, raw_conserved = raw_dg_state(integrator)
        grid_fields = interpolate_conserved_grid(raw_x, raw_y, raw_conserved)
        final_symmetry = mirror_symmetry_error(grid_fields)

        verification_latest[:time] = integrator.t
        verification_latest[:symmetry] = final_symmetry
        verification_latest[:final_symmetry] = final_symmetry
        println(Printf.@sprintf(
            "Final mirror symmetry at t=%.5f: eps_sym=%.6e " *
            "(acceptance <= 1.0e-10)",
            integrator.t, final_symmetry))

        isfinite(final_symmetry) && final_symmetry <= 1.0e-10 ||
            error("Final mirror-symmetry acceptance failed: " *
                  "eps_sym(3.0)=$final_symmetry exceeds 1e-10")
    end
    return nothing
end

symmetry_verification_callback = DiscreteCallback(
    (u, t, integrator) -> false,
    integrator -> nothing;
    initialize=initialize_symmetry_verification,
    finalize=finalize_symmetry_verification,
    save_positions=(false, false))

summary_callback = SummaryCallback()
analysis_interval = 100
analysis_callback = AnalysisCallback(semi, interval=analysis_interval)
alive_callback = AliveCallback(analysis_interval=analysis_interval)

# Save full-state snapshots at the required physical-time interval.
trixi_out = joinpath(@__DIR__, "trixi_output")
save_solution = SaveSolutionCallback(dt=dt_save,
                                     save_initial_solution=true,
                                     save_final_solution=true,
                                     output_directory=trixi_out,
                                     solution_variables=cons2prim)
stepsize_callback = StepsizeCallback(cfl=0.3)

callbacks = CallbackSet(summary_callback,
                        analysis_callback,
                        alive_callback,
                        save_solution,
                        amr_callback,
                        symmetry_verification_callback,
                        energy_balance_callback,
                        save_npz_callback,
                        stepsize_callback)
```

```julia
function final_diagnostic_value(name)
    value = get(verification_latest, name, NaN)
    isfinite(value) ||
        error("Final verification summary: missing or non-finite diagnostic $name")
    return value
end

function archived_spike_tip_at(times, positions, target_time)
    index = findfirst(
        time -> isapprox(time, target_time; atol=1.0e-10, rtol=0.0), times)
    index === nothing &&
        error("Spike-tip archive: missing save time $target_time")
    return positions[index]
end

function print_verification_summary()
    final_filename = joinpath(npz_out,
                              Printf.@sprintf("time_%08.5f.npz", 3.0))
    verify_npz_file(final_filename)
    final_data = npzread(final_filename)

    final_time = Float64(npz_scalar(final_data["t"]))
    isapprox(final_time, 3.0; atol=1.0e-10, rtol=0.0) ||
        error("Final verification summary: the final archive is not at t=3")

    # Recompute the metric from the archived common-grid conservative state.
    final_grids = (final_data["grid_rho"], final_data["grid_rho_u"],
                   final_data["grid_rho_v"], final_data["grid_energy"])
    final_symmetry = mirror_symmetry_error(final_grids)
    isfinite(final_symmetry) && final_symmetry <= 1.0e-10 ||
        error("Final mirror-symmetry acceptance failed during summary: " *
              "eps_sym(3.0)=$final_symmetry exceeds 1e-10")

    minimum_mass_error = get(verification_minima, :mass, NaN)
    isfinite(minimum_mass_error) ||
        error("Final verification summary: no finite mass diagnostic")

    final_mass_drift = final_diagnostic_value(:mass_drift)
    final_relative_mass_drift =
        final_diagnostic_value(:relative_mass_drift)
    initial_projection_error =
        final_diagnostic_value(:initial_projection_symmetry)
    source_error = final_diagnostic_value(:source_symmetry)
    wall_velocity = final_diagnostic_value(:wall_max_abs_v)
    energy_change = final_diagnostic_value(:energy_change)
    gravity_work = final_diagnostic_value(:cumulative_gravity_work)
    energy_residual = final_diagnostic_value(:energy_residual)
    energy_closure = final_diagnostic_value(:energy_closure)

    rho_min, rho_max = extrema(final_data["rho"])
    p_min, p_max = extrema(final_data["p"])
    rho_min > 0.0 && p_min > 0.0 ||
        error("Final verification summary: nonpositive density or pressure")

    # Confirm that the spike-tip track contains every common save time before
    # reporting the late-time positions needed by the next iteration.
    track_filename = joinpath(npz_out, "spike_tip_track.npz")
    isfile(track_filename) ||
        error("Final verification summary: missing spike_tip_track.npz")
    track_data = npzread(track_filename)
    haskey(track_data, "times") && haskey(track_data, "spike_tip") ||
        error("Final verification summary: incomplete spike-tip archive")

    track_times = Float64.(vec(track_data["times"]))
    track_positions = Float64.(vec(track_data["spike_tip"]))
    length(track_times) == length(archive_save_times) &&
        length(track_positions) == length(archive_save_times) ||
        error("Final verification summary: incomplete common-time archive")
    all(isfinite, track_times) && all(isfinite, track_positions) ||
        error("Final verification summary: non-finite spike-tip archive")

    for index in eachindex(archive_save_times)
        isapprox(track_times[index], archive_save_times[index];
                 atol=1.0e-10, rtol=0.0) ||
            error("Final verification summary: noncommon spike-tip save time " *
                  "$(track_times[index])")
    end

    spike_tip_2 = archived_spike_tip_at(track_times, track_positions, 2.0)
    spike_tip_3 = archived_spike_tip_at(track_times, track_positions, 3.0)

    println("Minimum achieved error: ",
            Printf.@sprintf("%.6e", minimum_mass_error),
            " (mass); ",
            Printf.@sprintf(
                "final_dM=%.6e, final_relative_dM=%.6e; ",
                final_mass_drift, final_relative_mass_drift),
            "mass-conservation diagnostic only; no exact RTI solution exists")
    println(Printf.@sprintf(
        "Symmetry: eps_sym(3.0)=%.6e (acceptance <= 1.0e-10), " *
        "eps_IC=%.6e, eps_S=%.6e",
        final_symmetry, initial_projection_error, source_error))
    println(Printf.@sprintf(
        "Positivity at t=3: rho=[%.6e,%.6e], p=[%.6e,%.6e]; " *
        "raw and common-grid NPZ positivity checks passed",
        rho_min, rho_max, p_min, p_max))
    println(Printf.@sprintf(
        "Wall no-penetration at t=3: max_wall_abs_v=%.6e",
        wall_velocity))
    println(Printf.@sprintf(
        "Energy balance at t=3: delta_E=%.6e, " *
        "cumulative_gravity_work=%.6e, energy_residual=%.6e, " *
        "energy_closure=%.6e",
        energy_change, gravity_work, energy_residual, energy_closure))
    println(Printf.@sprintf(
        "Spike-tip archive: samples=%d, spike_tip(2.0)=%.6e, " *
        "spike_tip(3.0)=%.6e",
        length(track_times), spike_tip_2, spike_tip_3))
    return nothing
end

println("Done.")
print_verification_summary()
```

 The code implements the specified nonlinear 2D compressible Rayleigh–Taylor instability as a numerical DGSEM simulation. Spatial discretization uses DGSEM polydeg=5, HLLC surface flux, Ranocha volume flux, Hennemann–Gassner shock capturing with density indicator and alpha_max=0.2, and local Lax–Friedrichs blending. The code also performs initial-projection and gravity-source mirror-symmetry checks, accumulates gravity work every accepted step, computes mass/positivity/wall/energy/symmetry diagnostics, archives NPZ files at dt_save=0.1, tracks the spike-tip position, and saves summary_all.png with rho and v panels at t=0.5,1.0,2.0,3.0 using colorbar=false.
Proposed name:CompressibleEuler_RayleighTaylor_Periodic_Reflective\Question :Let's solve the 2D Compressible Euler equations for the Rayleigh-Taylor Instability.

**Physics Parameters:**
* **Equations:** Compressible Euler ($\gamma = 1.4$).
* **Gravity:** $g = 1.0$ acting in the **positive $y$-direction** (The source term adds momentum in $+y$).
* **Inviscid:** $\mu = 0$, thermal conductivity $k = 0$.

**Domain:**
$(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$
Time interval: $t \in [0, 3.0]$

**Boundary Conditions:**
* $x$-direction: **Periodic**.
* $y$-direction: **Reflective / Free-slip walls** at $y=0$ and $y=1$ (No-penetration condition $v=0$).

**Initial Conditions:**
The domain is split at the interface $y=0.5$.
* **Density ($\rho$):** A smoothed interface between $\rho_{heavy} = 2.0$ (bottom) and $\rho_{light} = 1.0$ (top).
    $$ \rho(y) = \rho_{heavy} + (\rho_{light} - \rho_{heavy}) \cdot \frac{1}{2}\left(1 + \tanh\left(50(y - 0.5)\right)\right) $$
* **Pressure ($p$):** Hydrostatic balance ($\frac{dp}{dy} = \rho g$).
    $$ p(y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1 & \text{if } y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5 & \text{if } y \geq 0.5 \end{cases} $$
* **Velocity:** Initialized with zero horizontal velocity ($u=0$) and a perturbation in vertical velocity ($v$):
    $$ v(x,y) = -0.025 \cdot c \cdot \cos(8\pi x) \cdot \sin(\pi y)^6 $$
    Where $c = \sqrt{\gamma p / \rho}$ is the local speed of sound.

```julia
# Wrap the supported controller while forwarding its fields for AMRCallback.
# No abstract controller supertype is required for get_element_variables!
# dispatch or for the generic AMRCallback interface.
struct MirrorPairedAMRController{C}
    base_controller::C
end

function Base.getproperty(controller::MirrorPairedAMRController, name::Symbol)
    name === :base_controller && return getfield(controller, :base_controller)
    return getproperty(getfield(controller, :base_controller), name)
end
```
