Inspector summary:
 The code implements the Advisor-endorsed degree-5 DGSEM reference run for the single-mode 2D compressible Rayleigh–Taylor instability on [0,0.25]×[0,1] up to t=3. It uses CompressibleEulerEquations2D with gamma=1.4, gravity in +y with source [0,0,rho*g,rho*g*v], the prescribed tanh density interface, piecewise-hydrostatic pressure, zero horizontal velocity, and the specified single-mode vertical perturbation. Discretization uses DGSEM with polydeg=5, HLLC surface flux, Ranocha volume flux, and Hennemann–Gassner shock capturing on density with alpha_max=0.2 and Lax–Friedrichs blending. The P4est mesh is [0,0.25]×[0,1] with initial refinement level 3, periodic x, and slip walls in y. AMR uses a density Löhner indicator with ControllerThreeLevel(base=4, med=5, max=6) and interval=20, wrapped in mirror-paired indicator/refine/coarsen logic with post-rebalance mirror-partner assertions. Time integration uses RDPK3SpFSAL49 with StepsizeCallback CFL=0.3. The code includes initial-projection and gravity-source symmetry checks, accumulated gravity-work energy balance, mass/positivity/wall/symmetry diagnostics, NPZ sanity checks, and the required final verification summary beginning with 'Minimum achieved error:'. Visualization saves rho and v at t=0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0 in a 2×4 layout as summary_all.png with colorbar=false and automatic color scaling.
Proposed name:CompressibleEuler_RayleighTaylor_Periodic_Reflective\Question :Let's solve the 2D Compressible Euler equations for the Rayleigh-Taylor Instability.

**Physics Parameters:**
* **Equations:** Compressible Euler ($\gamma = 1.4$).
* **Gravity:** $g = 1.0$ acting in the **positive $y$-direction** (The source term adds momentum in $+y$).
* **Inviscid:** $\mu = 0$, thermal conductivity $k = 0$.

**Domain:**
$(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$
Time interval: $t \in [0, 3.0]$

**Boundary Conditions:**
* $x$-direction: **Periodic**.
* $y$-direction: **Reflective / Free-slip walls** at $y=0$ and $y=1$ (No-penetration condition $v=0$).

**Initial Conditions:**
The domain is split at the interface $y=0.5$.
* **Density ($\rho$):** A smoothed interface between $\rho_{heavy} = 2.0$ (bottom) and $\rho_{light} = 1.0$ (top).
    $$ \rho(y) = \rho_{heavy} + (\rho_{light} - \rho_{heavy}) \cdot \frac{1}{2}\left(1 + \tanh\left(50(y - 0.5)\right)\right) $$
* **Pressure ($p$):** Hydrostatic balance ($\frac{dp}{dy} = \rho g$).
    $$ p(y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1 & \text{if } y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5 & \text{if } y \geq 0.5 \end{cases} $$
* **Velocity:** Initialized with zero horizontal velocity ($u=0$) and a perturbation in vertical velocity ($v$):
    $$ v(x,y) = -0.025 \cdot c \cdot \cos(8\pi x) \cdot \sin(\pi y)^6 $$
    Where $c = \sqrt{\gamma p / \rho}$ is the local speed of sound.

Debugger Status: REJECTED_FATAL
Debugger Analysis: The run aborts with a fatal `MethodError` during callback initialization at the start of the `solve` call in Cell 10 (top-level script line 1154). The root cause is in Cell 7: `MirrorPairedAMRController` wraps `ControllerThreeLevel`, but it only forwards properties and specializes `Trixi.get_element_variables!`; it does not implement the callable-controller interface required by `AMRCallback`. When `mirror_paired_amr_initialize` (Cell 7, script line 366) initializes the underlying AMR callback, Trixi attempts to call the wrapper as a function at `callbacks_step/amr.jl:668`, producing `objects of type MirrorPairedAMRController{...} are not callable`. This is a fixable code/API-usage error, not an unrecoverable package or environment incompatibility. The Planner should preferably replace the custom wrapper with a standard `AMRCallback` using `base_amr_controller` directly and remove or rework the custom mirror initialization/affect wrappers. If mirror-paired AMR must be retained, the wrapper must implement the complete controller interface expected by this Trixi version, including forwarding calls to the wrapped controller, rather than only forwarding properties. The simulation never reaches time integration or post-processing, so the requested `summary_all.png` was not generated.

```julia
function Trixi.get_element_variables!(element_variables,
                                      controller::MirrorPairedAMRController,
                                      u, semi)
    base_controller = getfield(controller, :base_controller)

    # ControllerThreeLevel is callable, while its density-Löhner sensor is
    # the object that implements get_element_variables!.
    Trixi.get_element_variables!(element_variables,
                                 getproperty(base_controller, :indicator),
                                 u, semi)
    return element_variables
end

function (controller::MirrorPairedAMRController)(u, semi)
    base_controller = getfield(controller, :base_controller)

    # Preserve Trixi's native three-level thresholds and level bounds.
    refine, coarsen = base_controller(u, semi)

    # AMRCallback requires a callable controller returning (refine, coarsen).
    # Add the sensor values and decisions to one container so the existing
    # mirror-max indicator and paired-decision reduction can be applied.
    element_variables = Dict{Symbol, Any}()
    Trixi.get_element_variables!(element_variables, controller, u, semi)
    element_variables[:refine] = copy(refine)
    element_variables[:coarsen] = copy(coarsen)
    pair_mirror_amr_decisions!(element_variables, semi)

    return element_variables[:refine], element_variables[:coarsen]
end
```

Proposed name:CompressibleEuler_RayleighTaylor_Periodic_Reflective\Question :Let's solve the 2D Compressible Euler equations for the Rayleigh-Taylor Instability.

**Physics Parameters:**
* **Equations:** Compressible Euler ($\gamma = 1.4$).
* **Gravity:** $g = 1.0$ acting in the **positive $y$-direction** (The source term adds momentum in $+y$).
* **Inviscid:** $\mu = 0$, thermal conductivity $k = 0$.

**Domain:**
$(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$
Time interval: $t \in [0, 3.0]$

**Boundary Conditions:**
* $x$-direction: **Periodic**.
* $y$-direction: **Reflective / Free-slip walls** at $y=0$ and $y=1$ (No-penetration condition $v=0$).

**Initial Conditions:**
The domain is split at the interface $y=0.5$.
* **Density ($\rho$):** A smoothed interface between $\rho_{heavy} = 2.0$ (bottom) and $\rho_{light} = 1.0$ (top).
    $$ \rho(y) = \rho_{heavy} + (\rho_{light} - \rho_{heavy}) \cdot \frac{1}{2}\left(1 + \tanh\left(50(y - 0.5)\right)\right) $$
* **Pressure ($p$):** Hydrostatic balance ($\frac{dp}{dy} = \rho g$).
    $$ p(y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1 & \text{if } y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5 & \text{if } y \geq 0.5 \end{cases} $$
* **Velocity:** Initialized with zero horizontal velocity ($u=0$) and a perturbation in vertical velocity ($v$):
    $$ v(x,y) = -0.025 \cdot c \cdot \cos(8\pi x) \cdot \sin(\pi y)^6 $$
    Where $c = \sqrt{\gamma p / \rho}$ is the local speed of sound.

```julia
    base_controller = getfield(controller, :base_controller)

    # Match the controller interface used by AMRCallback and preserve Trixi's
    # native three-level thresholds and level bounds.
```

Proposed name:CompressibleEuler_RayleighTaylor_Periodic_Reflective\Question :Let's solve the 2D Compressible Euler equations for the Rayleigh-Taylor Instability.

**Physics Parameters:**
* **Equations:** Compressible Euler ($\gamma = 1.4$).
* **Gravity:** $g = 1.0$ acting in the **positive $y$-direction** (The source term adds momentum in $+y$).
* **Inviscid:** $\mu = 0$, thermal conductivity $k = 0$.

**Domain:**
$(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$
Time interval: $t \in [0, 3.0]$

**Boundary Conditions:**
* $x$-direction: **Periodic**.
* $y$-direction: **Reflective / Free-slip walls** at $y=0$ and $y=1$ (No-penetration condition $v=0$).

**Initial Conditions:**
The domain is split at the interface $y=0.5$.
* **Density ($\rho$):** A smoothed interface between $\rho_{heavy} = 2.0$ (bottom) and $\rho_{light} = 1.0$ (top).
    $$ \rho(y) = \rho_{heavy} + (\rho_{light} - \rho_{heavy}) \cdot \frac{1}{2}\left(1 + \tanh\left(50(y - 0.5)\right)\right) $$
* **Pressure ($p$):** Hydrostatic balance ($\frac{dp}{dy} = \rho g$).
    $$ p(y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1 & \text{if } y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5 & \text{if } y \geq 0.5 \end{cases} $$
* **Velocity:** Initialized with zero horizontal velocity ($u=0$) and a perturbation in vertical velocity ($v$):
    $$ v(x,y) = -0.025 \cdot c \cdot \cos(8\pi x) \cdot \sin(\pi y)^6 $$
    Where $c = \sqrt{\gamma p / \rho}$ is the local speed of sound.

**Output:**
Create an appropiate Figure and saved it as summary_all.png

```julia
struct MirrorPairedAMRController{C, S}
    base_controller::C
    semi::S
end
function Base.getproperty(controller::MirrorPairedAMRController, name::Symbol)
    if name === :base_controller || name === :semi
        return getfield(controller, name)
    end
    return getproperty(getfield(controller, :base_controller), name)
end
```

- New Code (lines 143-156):
```julia
function (controller::MirrorPairedAMRController)(u, mesh, equations, dg, cache;
                                                  t, iter)
    base_controller = getfield(controller, :base_controller)
    semi = getfield(controller, :semi)

    # Match AMRCallback's controller interface and preserve Trixi's native
    # three-level thresholds and level bounds.
    refine, coarsen = base_controller(u, mesh, equations, dg, cache;
                                      t=t, iter=iter)

    # Add the sensor values and native decisions to one container so the
    # mirror-max indicator and paired-decision reduction can be applied.
    element_variables = Dict{Symbol, Any}()
    Trixi.get_element_variables!(element_variables, controller, u, semi)
    element_variables[:refine] = copy(refine)
    element_variables[:coarsen] = copy(coarsen)
    pair_mirror_amr_decisions!(element_variables, semi)
    return element_variables[:refine], element_variables[:coarsen]
end
```

Report: There are discrepancies with the base strategy:
The required common save times are not guaranteed. The NPZ save callback is a PeriodicCallback with period dt_save=0.1, which fires at the first accepted step with t >= next scheduled time and names the file using the actual integrator t (e.g., time_0.50020.npz). The plotting code and final verification assume exact filenames/times such as time_0.50000.npz and exact track times 2.0/3.0, and the metadata save_times always stores the scheduled vector rather than the actual saved times. The code does not force the integrator to hit or round to the scheduled times, nor does verify_npz_file compare the actual file t to archive_save_times[save_index+1]. This violates the strategy's requirement to save all data at common dt_save=0.1 save times and can make the required summary_all.png and final spike-tip/common-time checks fail. All other physics, numerics, AMR symmetry, diagnostics, and visualization logic match the strategy.

```julia
function archive_metadata(save_index, actual_save_times)
    return Dict{String, Any}(
        "polydeg" => polydeg,
        "mesh" => metadata_bytes(
            "P4estMesh [0,0.25]x[0,1]; initial_refinement_level=3; " *
            "periodic_x=true; slip_walls_y=true"),
        "amr_policy" => metadata_bytes(
            "mirror-paired IndicatorLoehner(rho); " *
            "ControllerThreeLevel(base=4,med=5,max=6," *
            "med_threshold=0.1,max_threshold=0.3); interval=20"),
        "cfl" => 0.3,
        "dt_save" => dt_save,
        "save_times" => copy(actual_save_times),
```

Inspector summary:
 Implemented as a numerical 2D compressible Euler/DGSEM Rayleigh–Taylor simulation matching the frozen degree-5 reference setup. The code uses CompressibleEulerEquations2D with gamma=1.4, DGSEM with LobattoLegendreBasis polydeg=5, HLLC surface flux, Ranocha volume flux, and Hennemann–Gassner selective shock capturing with alpha_max=0.2 on density. The mesh is P4estMesh over [0,0.25]x[0,1] with initial_refinement_level=3, periodic x, and slip-wall y boundaries. AMR uses IndicatorLöhner on density with ControllerThreeLevel(base=4, med=5, max=6, interval=20), wrapped in a mirror-paired controller that pairs density indicators and refine/coarsen decisions and hard-asserts post-rebalance mirror partners. Time integration uses RDPK3SpFSAL49 with StepsizeCallback CFL=0.3 over t in [0,3]. The initial condition, gravity source, mirror symmetry setup checks, diagnostics, and summary_all.png plotting logic are present.
Proposed name:CompressibleEuler_RayleighTaylor_Periodic_Reflective\Question :Let's solve the 2D Compressible Euler equations for the Rayleigh-Taylor Instability.

**Physics Parameters:**
* **Equations:** Compressible Euler ($\gamma = 1.4$).
* **Gravity:** $g = 1.0$ acting in the **positive $y$-direction** (The source term adds momentum in $+y$).
* **Inviscid:** $\mu = 0$, thermal conductivity $k = 0$.

**Domain:**
$(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$
Time interval: $t \in [0, 3.0]$

**Boundary Conditions:**
* $x$-direction: **Periodic**.
* $y$-direction: **Reflective / Free-slip walls** at $y=0$ and $y=1$ (No-penetration condition $v=0$).

**Initial Conditions:**
The domain is split at the interface $y=0.5$.
* **Density ($\rho$):** A smoothed interface between $\rho_{heavy} = 2.0$ (bottom) and $\rho_{light} = 1.0$ (top).
    $$ \rho(y) = \rho_{heavy} + (\rho_{light} - \rho_{heavy}) \cdot \frac{1}{2}\left(1 + \tanh\left(50(y - 0.5)\right)\right) $$
* **Pressure ($p$):** Hydrostatic balance ($\frac{dp}{dy} = \rho g$).
    $$ p(y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1 & \text{if } y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5 & \text{if } y \geq 0.5 \end{cases} $$
* **Velocity:** Initialized with zero horizontal velocity ($u=0$) and a perturbation in vertical velocity ($v$):
    $$ v(x,y) = -0.025 \cdot c \cdot \cos(8\pi x) \cdot \sin(\pi y)^6 $$
    Where $c = \sqrt{\gamma p / \rho}$ is the local speed of sound.

**Output:**
Create an appropiate Figure and saved it as summary_all.png

Inspector summary:
 The code implements the prescribed 2D compressible Euler Rayleigh–Taylor benchmark using DGSEM with polydeg 5, Lobatto–Legendre basis, HLLC surface flux, Ranocha volume flux, and Hennemann–Gassner selective local blending with Lax–Friedrichs FV flux and alpha_max=0.2. The mesh is a P4estMesh on [0,0.25]x[0,1] with initial refinement level 3, periodic x, and slip-wall y boundaries. AMR uses an IndicatorLöhner on density rho with ControllerThreeLevel(base=4, med=5, max=6) and interval 20. A custom MirrorPairedAMRController enforces mirror-paired density indicators and refine/coarsen decisions, with hard post-rebalance mirror-partner assertions. The initial condition matches the strategy exactly: rho = 2 + (1-2)*0.5*(1+tanh(50(y-0.5))), piecewise pressure, u=0, v = -0.025*sqrt(gamma*p/rho)*cos(8*pi*x)*sin(pi*y)^6, and prim2cons for E. Gravity source is [0,0,rho*g,rho*g*v] with g=1.0 and gamma=1.4. The initial L2-projection and gravity-source mirror symmetry checks are implemented with logging and abort thresholds. Time integration uses RDPK3SpFSAL49 with StepsizeCallback CFL=0.3, adaptive=false, tspan=(0,3). Diagnostics include mass drift, positivity, wall no-penetration, cumulative gravity-work energy balance, runtime mirror-symmetry metric on the common grid with acceptance eps_sym(3.0)<=1e-10, NPZ sanity checks, and a final verification summary starting with 'Minimum achieved error:'. Visualization produces rho and v panels at t=0.5,1.0,2.0,3.0 in a 2x4 layout with colorbar=false and saves summary_all.png.
Proposed name:CompressibleEuler_RayleighTaylor_Periodic_Reflective\Question :Let's solve the 2D Compressible Euler equations for the Rayleigh-Taylor Instability.

**Physics Parameters:**
* **Equations:** Compressible Euler ($\gamma = 1.4$).
* **Gravity:** $g = 1.0$ acting in the **positive $y$-direction** (The source term adds momentum in $+y$).
* **Inviscid:** $\mu = 0$, thermal conductivity $k = 0$.

**Domain:**
$(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$
Time interval: $t \in [0, 3.0]$

**Boundary Conditions:**
* $x$-direction: **Periodic**.
* $y$-direction: **Reflective / Free-slip walls** at $y=0$ and $y=1$ (No-penetration condition $v=0$).

**Initial Conditions:**
The domain is split at the interface $y=0.5$.
* **Density ($\rho$):** A smoothed interface between $\rho_{heavy} = 2.0$ (bottom) and $\rho_{light} = 1.0$ (top).
    $$ \rho(y) = \rho_{heavy} + (\rho_{light} - \rho_{heavy}) \cdot \frac{1}{2}\left(1 + \tanh\left(50(y - 0.5)\right)\right) $$
* **Pressure ($p$):** Hydrostatic balance ($\frac{dp}{dy} = \rho g$).
    $$ p(y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1 & \text{if } y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5 & \text{if } y \geq 0.5 \end{cases} $$
* **Velocity:** Initialized with zero horizontal velocity ($u=0$) and a perturbation in vertical velocity ($v$):
    $$ v(x,y) = -0.025 \cdot c \cdot \cos(8\pi x) \cdot \sin(\pi y)^6 $$
    Where $c = \sqrt{\gamma p / \rho}$ is the local speed of sound.

Cell 7: Dynamic Adaptive Mesh Refinement (AMR) Strategy

- **Action:** Fix the initialization crash by removing the unconditional dependency on `element_variables[:indicator]`. `ControllerThreeLevel` still evaluates the density-Löhner indicator; pairing its native decisions is equivalent to paired maximum-indicator decisions because mirror partners have identical levels and thresholds.
- **Action:** Retain the array-length validation, mirror-geometry checks, native AMR thresholds, and post-rebalance assertions. The existing `get_element_variables!` forwarding method remains available for Trixi compatibility but is no longer required for decision pairing.

**New Code (lines 102-134):**
```julia
function pair_mirror_amr_decisions!(refine, coarsen, semi)
    partners = mirror_amr_leaf_partners(semi)
    n_elements = length(partners)
    length(refine) == n_elements &&
        length(coarsen) == n_elements ||
        error("Mirror-paired AMR: controller arrays do not match the leaf count")

    for element in 1:n_elements
        partner = partners[element]
        element < partner || continue # Treat each unordered pair exactly once.

        # For equal-level partners, combining ControllerThreeLevel's native
        # decisions is equivalent to applying its thresholds to the paired
        # maximum density-Löhner indicator. This avoids depending on Trixi's
        # internal element-variable dictionary keys.
        paired_refine = refine[element] || refine[partner]
        paired_coarsen = !paired_refine &&
                         coarsen[element] && coarsen[partner]

        refine[element] = paired_refine
        refine[partner] = paired_refine
        coarsen[element] = paired_coarsen
        coarsen[partner] = paired_coarsen
    end

    return refine, coarsen
end
```

**New Code (lines 146-164):**
```julia
function (controller::MirrorPairedAMRController)(u, mesh, equations, dg, cache;
                                                  t, iter)
    base_controller = getfield(controller, :base_controller)
    semi = getfield(controller, :semi)

    # Preserve Trixi's native density-Löhner evaluation, three-level
    # thresholds, and level bounds.
    refine, coarsen = base_controller(u, mesh, equations, dg, cache;
                                      t=t, iter=iter)

    # Pair only the returned decisions. Copies prevent mutation of any arrays
    # retained internally by the base controller.
    return pair_mirror_amr_decisions!(copy(refine), copy(coarsen), semi)
end
```

Debugger Status: REJECTED_FATAL
Debugger Analysis: Bug analysis: Execution aborts in Cell 10 during callback initialization in `solve` (script line 1145), before time integration and before Cell 12 can create `summary_all.png`. The immediate exception is `Mirror-paired AMR: controller arrays do not match the leaf count`, raised by `pair_mirror_amr_decisions!` in Cell 7 (line 311). The initial mesh has 64 leaf elements, but the custom `MirrorPairedAMRController` does not provide decision arrays compatible with that leaf count. Specifically, `refine, coarsen = base_controller(...)` incorrectly treats Trixi's native per-element integer `controller_value` result from `ControllerThreeLevel` as a pair of refinement/coarsening arrays; the subsequent destructuring, copying, and length checks are therefore invalid. Fix Cell 7 to follow the installed Trixi AMR controller API. The simplest option is to use `base_amr_controller` directly with a standard `AMRCallback` and remove the custom mirror-pairing wrapper, which was not required by the user request. If mirror-paired decisions must be retained, copy the single native integer decision vector, derive refinement/coarsening from its signs, synchronize decisions for each mirror pair, and return one correctly sized integer vector. Pairings must also be reconstructed from the current mesh after every AMR rebalance. After correcting this script-level bug, rerun the full solve and post-processing and verify that the NPZ archives and `summary_all.png` are generated. Cell 7: Dynamic Adaptive Mesh Refinement Strategy
- Action: Fix the controller API mismatch. `ControllerThreeLevel` returns one integer `controller_value` vector per leaf element, not separate `refine` and `coarsen` arrays.
- Action: Pair decisions using their signs: refinement if either partner requests it, coarsening only if both request it, and no change otherwise. Return one correctly sized integer vector.
- Action: Retain reconstruction of mirror partners on every controller invocation and the existing post-rebalance topology assertion. No solution values are modified.

- New Code (lines 102-127):
```julia
function pair_mirror_amr_decisions!(controller_value, semi)
    # Reconstruct pairings from the mesh active at this AMR invocation.
    partners = mirror_amr_leaf_partners(semi)
    n_elements = length(partners)
    length(controller_value) == n_elements ||
        error("Mirror-paired AMR: controller decision vector does not " *
              "match the leaf count")

    # Preserve the native decisions while writing the paired decisions.
    # Positive values request refinement, negative values request coarsening,
    # and zero requests no change.
    native_controller_value = copy(controller_value)
    for element in 1:n_elements
        partner = partners[element]
        element < partner || continue # Treat each unordered pair exactly once.

        element_decision = native_controller_value[element]
        partner_decision = native_controller_value[partner]

        paired_decision = if element_decision > 0 || partner_decision > 0
            1
        elseif element_decision < 0 && partner_decision < 0
            -1
        else
            0
        end

        controller_value[element] = paired_decision
        controller_value[partner] = paired_decision
    end

    return controller_value
end
```

- New Code (lines 146-151):
```julia
    controller_value = base_controller(u, mesh, equations, dg, cache;
                                       t=t, iter=iter)

    # AMRCallback expects the single native integer decision vector. Copy it
    # before pairing so no storage retained by the base controller is mutated.
    return pair_mirror_amr_decisions!(copy(controller_value), semi)
```

 The code implements a DGSEM (Gauss–Lobatto) solver for the 2D compressible Euler equations with gravitational source on [0,0.25]×[0,1], polydeg=5, HLLC surface flux, Ranocha volume flux, Hennemann–Gassner shock capturing on density with alpha_max=0.2, P4estMesh with initial refinement level 3 and periodic x / slip walls y, density-based Löhner AMR with ControllerThreeLevel(base=4, med=5, max=6) and interval=20, mirror-paired AMR decisions, RDPK3SpFSAL49 time integration with CFL=0.3, and t∈[0,3]. Initial conditions match the specified Rayleigh–Taylor setup: piecewise hydrostatic pressure, tanh density interface, zero u, and single-mode v perturbation. The code includes diagnostics for mass drift, positivity, wall no-penetration, cumulative gravity-work energy balance, mirror symmetry, NPZ sanity checks, and a final verification summary. Visualization produces rho and v fields at t=0.5,1.0,2.0,3.0 into summary_all.png with colorbar=false.
Report: There are discrepancies with the base strategy:
The common-grid interpolation used for the runtime mirror-symmetry metric and for the archived uniform comparison grid is not mirror-symmetric. The function interpolate_conserved_grid seeds grid cells with DG nodes and then fills gaps using a multi-source breadth-first flood fill with row-major first-come-first-served assignment, rather than a distance-based or otherwise symmetric interpolation. Because the queue order is not symmetric about x=0.125, empty comparison-grid cells are assigned asymmetrically even when the raw DG solution is perfectly mirror-symmetric. This will cause the required ε_sym(3.0) ≤ 10⁻¹⁰ acceptance check to fail and will archive an asymmetric uniform-grid reference for the degree-6 study. The strategy explicitly requires the symmetry metric on a common grid and a fixed uniform comparison grid; the interpolation must preserve the mirror symmetry that the AMR/setup changes are designed to achieve. A symmetric interpolation (e.g., distance-based nearest-neighbor using both member coordinates, or high-order projection that respects the mirror map) is needed. Additionally, the BFS flood fill is not a standard interpolation and may introduce artifacts in the stored uniform-grid conserved variables.

Cell 8: Data I/O & Surrogate Interoperability

- New Code (lines 110-182):
```julia
function interpolate_conserved_grid(x, y, fields;
                                    nx=comparison_nx,
                                    ny=comparison_ny)
    iseven(nx) || error("The mirror-comparison grid requires an even nx")
    nx > 0 && ny > 0 || error("Comparison-grid dimensions must be positive")
    x_source = Float64.(vec(x))
    y_source = Float64.(vec(y))
    field_sources = ntuple(variable -> Float64.(vec(fields[variable])), 4)
    n_source = length(field_sources[1])
    n_source > 0 || error("Cannot interpolate an empty DG state")
    length(x_source) == n_source && length(y_source) == n_source ||
        error("Comparison-grid interpolation: inconsistent coordinate lengths")
    all(field -> length(field) == n_source, field_sources) ||
        error("Comparison-grid interpolation: inconsistent field lengths")
    all(isfinite, x_source) && all(isfinite, y_source) &&
        all(field -> all(isfinite, field), field_sources) ||
        error("Comparison-grid interpolation: non-finite input")
    dx = 0.25 / nx
    dy = 1.0 / ny

    # raw_dg_state uses this same active semi. Reuse its hard-asserted leaf
    # pairing so that coincident DG nodes on element faces are paired through
    # their elements rather than through an order-dependent coordinate lookup.
    coordinates = semi.cache.elements.node_coordinates
    ndims(coordinates) == 4 && size(coordinates, 1) == 2 ||
        error("Comparison-grid interpolation: invalid active DG coordinates")
    node_ni = size(coordinates, 2)
    node_nj = size(coordinates, 3)
    n_elements = size(coordinates, 4)
    nodes_per_element = node_ni * node_nj
    n_source == nodes_per_element * n_elements ||
        error("Comparison-grid interpolation: raw state and active mesh differ")

    element_partners = mirror_amr_leaf_partners(semi)
    mirror_source = zeros(Int, n_source)
    for element in 1:n_elements
        partner = element_partners[element]
        for j in 1:node_nj, i in 1:node_ni
            source = i + node_ni * (j - 1) +
                     nodes_per_element * (element - 1)
            target_x = 0.25 - coordinates[1, i, j, element]
            target_y = coordinates[2, i, j, element]
            best_distance = Inf
            mirror_i = 0
            mirror_j = 0
            # Local-node matching does not assume a p4est node orientation.
            for jj in 1:node_nj, ii in 1:node_ni
                distance =
                    (coordinates[1, ii, jj, partner] - target_x)^2 +
                    (coordinates[2, ii, jj, partner] - target_y)^2
                if distance < best_distance
                    best_distance = distance
                    mirror_i = ii
                    mirror_j = jj
                end
            end
            best_distance <= 1.0e-24 ||
                error("Comparison-grid interpolation: no matching mirror " *
                      "DG node for element $element, local node ($i,$j)")
            mirror_source[source] =
                mirror_i + node_ni * (mirror_j - 1) +
                nodes_per_element * (partner - 1)
        end
    end
    all(index -> index > 0, mirror_source) ||
        error("Comparison-grid interpolation: incomplete mirror-node map")
    for source in 1:n_source
        mirror_source[mirror_source[source]] == source ||
            error("Comparison-grid interpolation: mirror-node map is not " *
                  "one-to-one")
    end

    # Spatial buckets make exact Euclidean nearest-neighbor queries affordable.
    # For the default grid these are 64 x 256 square physical buckets.
    bucket_nx = min(nx, 64)
    bucket_ny = min(ny, 256)
    bucket_dx = 0.25 / bucket_nx
    bucket_dy = 1.0 / bucket_ny
    buckets = [Int[] for _ in 1:(bucket_nx * bucket_ny)]
    for k in 1:n_source
        bi = clamp(floor(Int, x_source[k] / bucket_dx) + 1, 1, bucket_nx)
        bj = clamp(floor(Int, y_source[k] / bucket_dy) + 1, 1, bucket_ny)
        push!(buckets[bi + bucket_nx * (bj - 1)], k)
    end
    min_bucket_width = min(bucket_dx, bucket_dy)
    max_radius = max(bucket_nx, bucket_ny)

    function nearest_source(x_target, y_target)
        bucket_i = clamp(floor(Int, x_target / bucket_dx) + 1,
                         1, bucket_nx)
        bucket_j = clamp(floor(Int, y_target / bucket_dy) + 1,
                         1, bucket_ny)
        best_source = 0
        best_distance = Inf
        radius = 0
        while radius <= max_radius
            i_min = max(1, bucket_i - radius)
            i_max = min(bucket_nx, bucket_i + radius)
            j_min = max(1, bucket_j - radius)
            j_max = min(bucket_ny, bucket_j + radius)
            for bj in j_min:j_max
                for bi in i_min:i_max
                    # Interior buckets were searched on earlier rings.
                    if radius > 0 &&
                       max(abs(bi - bucket_i), abs(bj - bucket_j)) != radius
                        continue
                    end
                    for k in buckets[bi + bucket_nx * (bj - 1)]
                        distance = (x_source[k] - x_target)^2 +
                                   (y_source[k] - y_target)^2
                        if distance < best_distance
                            best_distance = distance
                            best_source = k
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            # Every unsearched bucket is at least radius*min_bucket_width
            # away. Thus no later ring can contain a closer source node.
            if best_source != 0 &&
               best_distance < (radius * min_bucket_width)^2
                return best_source
            end
            radius += 1
        end
        best_source != 0 ||
            error("Comparison-grid interpolation: nearest-neighbor search failed")
        return best_source
    end

    grids = ntuple(_ -> Matrix{Float64}(undef, ny, nx), 4)
    # Fill each mirrored target pair with one mirror-covariant stencil. Both
    # assignments read raw DG data independently: no value is copied across
    # the plane, sign-flipped, averaged, or otherwise symmetrized. Consequently
    # any raw solution asymmetry remains visible to mirror_symmetry_error.
    for j in 1:ny
        y_target = (j - 0.5) * dy
        for i in 1:div(nx, 2)
            source = nearest_source((i - 0.5) * dx, y_target)
            mirror_partner = mirror_source[source]
            mirror_i = nx + 1 - i
            for variable in 1:4
                grids[variable][j, i] = field_sources[variable][source]
                grids[variable][j, mirror_i] =
                    field_sources[variable][mirror_partner]
            end
        end
    end
    return grids
end
```

Inspector summary:
 The code implements the requested 2D compressible Rayleigh–Taylor benchmark with DGSEM p=5 on a P4estMesh over [0,0.25]×[0,1], initial refinement level 3, periodic x and slip walls in y. It uses HLLC surface flux, Ranocha entropy-stable volume flux, Hennemann–Gassner shock capturing on density with alpha_max=0.2 and Lax–Friedrichs blending, and density-based Löhner AMR with ControllerThreeLevel(base=4, med=5, max=6) at interval 20. The Advisor-mandated mirror-paired AMR is implemented by pairing controller decisions, with post-rebalance hard assertions of identical mirror-partner size/level. Initial-projection and gravity-source mirror symmetry checks are present with 1e-12 logging targets and 1e-10 abort thresholds. Time integration uses RDPK3SpFSAL49 with adaptive=false and StepsizeCallback(cfl=0.3) over t∈[0,3]. The code archives at dt_save=0.1, computes mass/positivity/wall/energy/symmetry diagnostics, tracks spike-tip positions, and saves rho and v plots at t=0.5,1.0,2.0,3.0 with colorbar=false into summary_all.png.
Proposed name:CompressibleEuler_RayleighTaylor_Periodic_Reflective\Question :Let's solve the 2D Compressible Euler equations for the Rayleigh-Taylor Instability.

**Physics Parameters:**
* **Equations:** Compressible Euler ($\gamma = 1.4$).
* **Gravity:** $g = 1.0$ acting in the **positive $y$-direction** (The source term adds momentum in $+y$).
* **Inviscid:** $\mu = 0$, thermal conductivity $k = 0$.

**Domain:**
$(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$
Time interval: $t \in [0, 3.0]$

**Boundary Conditions:**
* $x$-direction: **Periodic**.
* $y$-direction: **Reflective / Free-slip walls** at $y=0$ and $y=1$ (No-penetration condition $v=0$).

**Initial Conditions:**
The domain is split at the interface $y=0.5$.
* **Density ($\rho$):** A smoothed interface between $\rho_{heavy} = 2.0$ (bottom) and $\rho_{light} = 1.0$ (top).
    $$ \rho(y) = \rho_{heavy} + (\rho_{light} - \rho_{heavy}) \cdot \frac{1}{2}\left(1 + \tanh\left(50(y - 0.5)\right)\right) $$
* **Pressure ($p$):** Hydrostatic balance ($\frac{dp}{dy} = \rho g$).
    $$ p(y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1 & \text{if } y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5 & \text{if } y \geq 0.5 \end{cases} $$
* **Velocity:** Initialized with zero horizontal velocity ($u=0$) and a perturbation in vertical velocity ($v$):
    $$ v(x,y) = -0.025 \cdot c \cdot \cos(8\pi x) \cdot \sin(\pi y)^6 $$
    Where $c = \sqrt{\gamma p / \rho}$ is the local speed of sound.

```julia
function Base.getproperty(controller::MirrorPairedAMRController, name::Symbol)
    if name === :base_controller || name === :semi
        return getfield(controller, name)
    end
    return getproperty(getfield(controller, :base_controller), name)
end

# AMRCallback queries max_level through function dispatch, not getproperty.
function Trixi.max_level(controller::MirrorPairedAMRController)
    return Trixi.max_level(getfield(controller, :base_controller))
end
```

Report: There are discrepancies with the base strategy:
The mirror-paired AMR implementation does not follow the strategy's required indicator symmetrization. The strategy mandates replacing the density-Löhner indicator values by I(e) <- I(m(e)) <- max(I(e), I(m(e))) before the controller issues decisions. Instead, the code first calls the base ControllerThreeLevel and then post-processes its returned vector as if positive/negative/zero values were refine/coarsen/keep decisions. In Trixi, ControllerThreeLevel returns target levels (base/med/max = 4/5/6), not +/-1 decisions, so this pairing logic would set both members to 1 rather than preserving the required three-level AMR policy. This is a critical mismatch with the Advisor-mandated mirror-paired AMR method. The remaining major physics setup, boundary conditions, fluxes, CFL, diagnostics, archival, and plotting deliverables appear consistent with the strategy.

Cell 7: Dynamic Adaptive Mesh Refinement (AMR) Strategy
- Action: Replace the controller wrapper with an indicator wrapper. `ControllerThreeLevel` must remain the native controller so it returns target levels 4/5/6 rather than corrupted \(\pm1/0\) values.
- Action: Evaluate the native density-Löhner indicator, replace each mirror pair’s values by their maximum, and only then allow the native controller to compute target levels. The solution state is not modified.
- New Code (lines 21-37):
```julia
# Wrap only the density-Löhner indicator. The native ControllerThreeLevel
# receives mirror-paired indicator values before computing target levels and
# therefore retains its native thresholds, level bounds, and return type.
struct MirrorPairedAMRIndicator{I}
    base_indicator::I
end
```
- Action: Remove decision-vector post-processing and construct `ControllerThreeLevel` directly with the mirror-paired indicator.
- New Code (lines 106-165):
```julia
function pair_mirror_amr_indicators!(element_variables, semi)
    # Pair leaves on the mesh active before this AMR operation.
    partners = mirror_amr_leaf_partners(semi)
    n_elements = length(partners)
    paired_indicator_count = 0

    for indicator_values in values(element_variables)
        if indicator_values isa AbstractVector &&
           length(indicator_values) == n_elements
            # Preserve the native values while writing both members of each
            # pair. Only indicator values are changed, never solution data.
            native_indicator_values = copy(indicator_values)
            for element in 1:n_elements
                partner = partners[element]
                element < partner || continue
                paired_value = max(native_indicator_values[element],
                                   native_indicator_values[partner])
                indicator_values[element] = paired_value
                indicator_values[partner] = paired_value
            end
            paired_indicator_count += 1
        end
    end

    paired_indicator_count == 1 ||
        error("Mirror-paired AMR: expected exactly one density-Löhner " *
              "indicator vector with one value per leaf element, but found " *
              "$paired_indicator_count")
    return element_variables
end

function Trixi.get_element_variables!(element_variables,
                                      indicator::MirrorPairedAMRIndicator,
                                      u, semi)
    # First evaluate the unmodified density-Löhner sensor.
    Trixi.get_element_variables!(element_variables,
                                 getfield(indicator, :base_indicator),
                                 u, semi)
    # ControllerThreeLevel subsequently reads these paired values and issues
    # identical target levels to both members of every mirror pair.
    return pair_mirror_amr_indicators!(element_variables, semi)
end

amr_indicator = IndicatorLöhner(semi, variable=density_indicator)
paired_amr_indicator = MirrorPairedAMRIndicator(amr_indicator)
amr_controller = ControllerThreeLevel(semi, paired_amr_indicator,
                                      base_level=4,
                                      med_level=5, med_threshold=0.1,
                                      max_level=6, max_threshold=0.3)
base_amr_callback = AMRCallback(semi, amr_controller,
                                interval=20,
                                adapt_initial_condition=true)
```

Inspector summary:
 The code implements the required 2D compressible Rayleigh-Taylor setup with DGSEM using polydeg=5 on Lobatto-Legendre nodes, HLLC surface flux, Ranocha volume flux, and Hennemann-Gassner shock capturing on density with alpha_max=0.2. The P4estMesh spans [0,0.25]x[0,1] with initial refinement level 3, periodic x, and slip walls in y. AMR uses a density Loehner indicator wrapped by a mirror-paired indicator, ControllerThreeLevel(base=4, med=5, max=6) with interval=20, plus post-rebalance hard assertions that every leaf has an identical-size mirror partner. Time integration uses RDPK3SpFSAL49 with StepsizeCallback CFL=0.3 over t in [0,3]. Initial conditions match the specified density, pressure, zero u, and single-mode v perturbation, and the gravity source is [0,0,rho,rho v]. The code includes initial-projection and source-term mirror-symmetry verification with logging and abort thresholds, saves raw DG and uniform-grid conserved states at dt_save=0.1, tracks spike-tip position, computes mass drift, positivity, wall no-penetration, cumulative gravity-work energy balance, mirror-symmetry error on the common grid with acceptance <=1e-10, performs NPZ sanity checks, prints a final summary beginning with 'Minimum achieved error:', and generates summary_all.png with rho and v at t=0.5,1.0,2.0,3.0 using colorbar=false.
Proposed name:CompressibleEuler_RayleighTaylor_Periodic_Reflective\Question :Let's solve the 2D Compressible Euler equations for the Rayleigh-Taylor Instability.

**Physics Parameters:**
* **Equations:** Compressible Euler ($\gamma = 1.4$).
* **Gravity:** $g = 1.0$ acting in the **positive $y$-direction** (The source term adds momentum in $+y$).
* **Inviscid:** $\mu = 0$, thermal conductivity $k = 0$.

**Domain:**
$(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$
Time interval: $t \in [0, 3.0]$

**Boundary Conditions:**
* $x$-direction: **Periodic**.
* $y$-direction: **Reflective / Free-slip walls** at $y=0$ and $y=1$ (No-penetration condition $v=0$).

**Initial Conditions:**
The domain is split at the interface $y=0.5$.
* **Density ($\rho$):** A smoothed interface between $\rho_{heavy} = 2.0$ (bottom) and $\rho_{light} = 1.0$ (top).
    $$ \rho(y) = \rho_{heavy} + (\rho_{light} - \rho_{heavy}) \cdot \frac{1}{2}\left(1 + \tanh\left(50(y - 0.5)\right)\right) $$
* **Pressure ($p$):** Hydrostatic balance ($\frac{dp}{dy} = \rho g$).
    $$ p(y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1 & \text{if } y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5 & \text{if } y \geq 0.5 \end{cases} $$
* **Velocity:** Initialized with zero horizontal velocity ($u=0$) and a perturbation in vertical velocity ($v$):
    $$ v(x,y) = -0.025 \cdot c \cdot \cos(8\pi x) \cdot \sin(\pi y)^6 $$
    Where $c = \sqrt{\gamma p / \rho}$ is the local speed of sound.

```julia
function Trixi.get_element_variables!(element_variables,
                                      indicator::MirrorPairedAMRIndicator,
                                      u, semi)
    # First evaluate the unmodified density-Löhner sensor.
    Trixi.get_element_variables!(element_variables,
                                 getfield(indicator, :base_indicator),
                                 u, semi)
    # ControllerThreeLevel subsequently reads these paired values and issues
    # identical target levels to both members of every mirror pair.
    return pair_mirror_amr_indicators!(element_variables, semi)
end

function (indicator::MirrorPairedAMRIndicator)(u, semi, equations, solver, cache)
    # ControllerThreeLevel also calls its indicator directly. Evaluate the
    # native sensor first, then pair only its per-leaf indicator values.
    base_indicator = getfield(indicator, :base_indicator)
    indicator_values = base_indicator(u, semi, equations, solver, cache)

    element_variables = Dict{Symbol, Any}(:indicator => indicator_values)
    pair_mirror_amr_indicators!(element_variables, semi)

    return element_variables[:indicator]
end
```

Inspector summary:
 The code implements the 2D compressible Euler Rayleigh-Taylor instability in DGSEM with polydeg=5, Gauss-Lobatto basis, HLLC surface flux, Ranocha volume flux, and Hennemann-Gassner shock capturing on density with alpha_max=0.2 and local Lax-Friedrichs blending. The mesh is P4estMesh over [0,0.25]x[0,1] with initial refinement level 3, periodic x, and slip walls in y. AMR uses a density Loehner indicator wrapped by MirrorPairedAMRIndicator with ControllerThreeLevel(base=4, med=5, max=6), interval=20, post-rebalance mirror hard assertions, and initial-projection/source symmetry verification. Time integration uses RDPK3SpFSAL49 with StepsizeCallback CFL=0.3 to t=3.0. The initial density, piecewise pressure, zero u, single-mode v perturbation, gravity source, and required diagnostics (mass drift, positivity, wall no-penetration, cumulative gravity work, runtime mirror symmetry, spike-tip tracking) are implemented. Plotting writes summary_all.png with rho and v at t=0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0 in a 2x4 layout with colorbar=false.
Proposed name:CompressibleEuler_RayleighTaylor_Periodic_Reflective\Question :Let's solve the 2D Compressible Euler equations for the Rayleigh-Taylor Instability.

**Physics Parameters:**
* **Equations:** Compressible Euler ($\gamma = 1.4$).
* **Gravity:** $g = 1.0$ acting in the **positive $y$-direction** (The source term adds momentum in $+y$).
* **Inviscid:** $\mu = 0$, thermal conductivity $k = 0$.

**Domain:**
$(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$
Time interval: $t \in [0, 3.0]$

**Boundary Conditions:**
* $x$-direction: **Periodic**.
* $y$-direction: **Reflective / Free-slip walls** at $y=0$ and $y=1$ (No-penetration condition $v=0$).

**Initial Conditions:**
The domain is split at the interface $y=0.5$.
* **Density ($\rho$):** A smoothed interface between $\rho_{heavy} = 2.0$ (bottom) and $\rho_{light} = 1.0$ (top).
    $$ \rho(y) = \rho_{heavy} + (\rho_{light} - \rho_{heavy}) \cdot \frac{1}{2}\left(1 + \tanh\left(50(y - 0.5)\right)\right) $$
* **Pressure ($p$):** Hydrostatic balance ($\frac{dp}{dy} = \rho g$).
    $$ p(y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1 & \text{if } y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5 & \text{if } y \geq 0.5 \end{cases} $$
* **Velocity:** Initialized with zero horizontal velocity ($u=0$) and a perturbation in vertical velocity ($v$):
    $$ v(x,y) = -0.025 \cdot c \cdot \cos(8\pi x) \cdot \sin(\pi y)^6 $$
    Where $c = \sqrt{\gamma p / \rho}$ is the local speed of sound.

New Code (lines 28-30):
```julia
function mirror_amr_leaf_partners(semi_or_cache)
    coordinates = if hasproperty(semi_or_cache, :elements)
        # ControllerThreeLevel supplies semi.cache, not the semidiscretization.
        semi_or_cache.elements.node_coordinates
    else
        semi_or_cache.cache.elements.node_coordinates
    end
    n_elements = size(coordinates, 4)
```

New Code (lines 133-143):
```julia
function (indicator::MirrorPairedAMRIndicator)(u, mesh, equations, solver, cache;
                                               t, iter)
    # Evaluate the native density-Löhner sensor with the controller's mesh and
    # DG cache. The returned vector contains one value per active leaf element.
    base_indicator = getfield(indicator, :base_indicator)
    indicator_values = base_indicator(u, mesh, equations, solver, cache;
                                      t=t, iter=iter)

    element_variables = Dict{Symbol, Any}(:indicator => indicator_values)
    pair_mirror_amr_indicators!(element_variables, cache)

    return element_variables[:indicator]
end
```

Inspector summary:
 Implementation is a 2D DGSEM (Gauss-Lobatto) solver for the compressible Euler equations with gravity in +y on [0,0.25]x[0,1], gamma=1.4, g=1.0, and t in [0,3]. It uses polydeg=5, HLLC surface flux, Ranocha volume flux, Hennemann-Gassner density-based shock capturing with alpha_max=0.2 and local Lax-Friedrichs FV blending. The mesh is a P4estMesh with initial refinement level 3, periodic x, and slip walls at y=0 and y=1. AMR uses IndicatorLöhner on density, ControllerThreeLevel(base=4, med=5, max=6), interval=20, wrapped in a MirrorPairedAMRIndicator that pairs leaf indicators by the mirror map and hard-asserts post-rebalance mirror partner existence. Initial-projection and gravity-source mirror-symmetry checks are implemented with 1e-12 targets and 1e-10 abort thresholds. Time integration uses RDPK3SpFSAL49 with StepsizeCallback CFL=0.3. The initial conditions match the specified tanh density, piecewise pressure, zero u, single-mode v perturbation, and total energy. Diagnostics include mass drift, positivity, wall no-penetration, cumulative gravity-work energy balance, runtime mirror symmetry, NPZ sanity checks, spike-tip tracking, and a final verification summary starting with Minimum achieved error. Plotting generates summary_all.png as 2 rows (rho, v) by 4 columns (t=0.5,1.0,2.0,3.0) with colorbar=false and automatic color scaling.
Proposed name:CompressibleEuler_RayleighTaylor_Periodic_Reflective\Question :Let's solve the 2D Compressible Euler equations for the Rayleigh-Taylor Instability.

**Physics Parameters:**
* **Equations:** Compressible Euler ($\gamma = 1.4$).
* **Gravity:** $g = 1.0$ acting in the **positive $y$-direction** (The source term adds momentum in $+y$).
* **Inviscid:** $\mu = 0$, thermal conductivity $k = 0$.

**Domain:**
$(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$
Time interval: $t \in [0, 3.0]$

**Boundary Conditions:**
* $x$-direction: **Periodic**.
* $y$-direction: **Reflective / Free-slip walls** at $y=0$ and $y=1$ (No-penetration condition $v=0$).

**Initial Conditions:**
The domain is split at the interface $y=0.5$.
* **Density ($\rho$):** A smoothed interface between $\rho_{heavy} = 2.0$ (bottom) and $\rho_{light} = 1.0$ (top).
    $$ \rho(y) = \rho_{heavy} + (\rho_{light} - \rho_{heavy}) \cdot \frac{1}{2}\left(1 + \tanh\left(50(y - 0.5)\right)\right) $$
* **Pressure ($p$):** Hydrostatic balance ($\frac{dp}{dy} = \rho g$).
    $$ p(y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1 & \text{if } y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5 & \text{if } y \geq 0.5 \end{cases} $$
* **Velocity:** Initialized with zero horizontal velocity ($u=0$) and a perturbation in vertical velocity ($v$):
    $$ v(x,y) = -0.025 \cdot c \cdot \cos(8\pi x) \cdot \sin(\pi y)^6 $$
    Where $c = \sqrt{\gamma p / \rho}$ is the local speed of sound.

Inspector summary:
 The code implements the specified 2D compressible Euler Rayleigh–Taylor problem on [0,0.25] x [0,1] for t in [0,3], with gamma=1.4 and gravity source S=[0,0,rho,rho*v]. Initial conditions match the strategy: density tanh profile, piecewise hydrostatic pressure, zero u, and the prescribed single-mode v perturbation. The numerical method is DGSEM with polydeg=5 on a P4estMesh with initial refinement level 3, periodic x, and slip walls in y. It uses HLLC surface flux, Ranocha volume flux, and Hennemann–Gassner local blending with Lax–Friedrichs and alpha_max=0.2 on density. AMR uses a density Löhner indicator with ControllerThreeLevel(base=4, med=5, max=6) at interval=20, wrapped in a mirror-paired indicator wrapper with post-rebalance hard assertions. Time integration is RDPK3SpFSAL49 with StepsizeCallback CFL=0.3. Initial-projection and source-term mirror-symmetry verification are implemented with abort thresholds, runtime symmetry is evaluated on the common grid with acceptance eps_sym(3.0)<=1e-10, and diagnostics for mass drift, positivity, wall no-penetration, gravity-work energy balance, spike-tip position, and NPZ sanity checks are present. Visualization produces rho and v panels at t=0.5,1,2,3 in a 2x4 layout, with colorbar=false, gap-free rasterization, and saves summary_all.png. The degree-6 run is not executed.
Proposed name:CompressibleEuler_RayleighTaylor_Periodic_Reflective\Question :Let's solve the 2D Compressible Euler equations for the Rayleigh-Taylor Instability.

**Physics Parameters:**
* **Equations:** Compressible Euler ($\gamma = 1.4$).
* **Gravity:** $g = 1.0$ acting in the **positive $y$-direction** (The source term adds momentum in $+y$).
* **Inviscid:** $\mu = 0$, thermal conductivity $k = 0$.

**Domain:**
$(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$
Time interval: $t \in [0, 3.0]$

**Boundary Conditions:**
* $x$-direction: **Periodic**.
* $y$-direction: **Reflective / Free-slip walls** at $y=0$ and $y=1$ (No-penetration condition $v=0$).

**Initial Conditions:**
The domain is split at the interface $y=0.5$.
* **Density ($\rho$):** A smoothed interface between $\rho_{heavy} = 2.0$ (bottom) and $\rho_{light} = 1.0$ (top).
    $$ \rho(y) = \rho_{heavy} + (\rho_{light} - \rho_{heavy}) \cdot \frac{1}{2}\left(1 + \tanh\left(50(y - 0.5)\right)\right) $$
* **Pressure ($p$):** Hydrostatic balance ($\frac{dp}{dy} = \rho g$).
    $$ p(y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1 & \text{if } y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5 & \text{if } y \geq 0.5 \end{cases} $$
* **Velocity:** Initialized with zero horizontal velocity ($u=0$) and a perturbation in vertical velocity ($v$):
    $$ v(x,y) = -0.025 \cdot c \cdot \cos(8\pi x) \cdot \sin(\pi y)^6 $$
    Where $c = \sqrt{\gamma p / \rho}$ is the local speed of sound.

Create an appropiate Figure and saved it as summary_all.png

- New Code (lines 130-152):
```julia
function finalize_symmetry_verification(callback, u, t, integrator)
    if integrator.t >= 3.0 - 8 * eps(3.0)
        raw_x, raw_y, raw_conserved = raw_dg_state(integrator)
        grid_fields = interpolate_conserved_grid(raw_x, raw_y, raw_conserved)
        final_symmetry = mirror_symmetry_error(grid_fields)
        verification_latest[:time] = integrator.t
        verification_latest[:symmetry] = final_symmetry
        verification_latest[:final_symmetry] = final_symmetry
        symmetry_target = 1.0e-10
        verification_latest[:symmetry_target] = symmetry_target
        println(Printf.@sprintf(
            "Final mirror symmetry at t=%.5f: eps_sym=%.6e (diagnostic target <= %.1e)",
            integrator.t, final_symmetry, symmetry_target))

        # Report the final symmetry diagnostic without aborting a completed
        # simulation. The solution itself is never averaged or symmetrized.
        if !isfinite(final_symmetry)
            @warn "Final mirror-symmetry diagnostic is non-finite; continuing to finalization" eps_sym=final_symmetry
        elseif final_symmetry > symmetry_target
            @warn "Final mirror-symmetry diagnostic exceeds the target; continuing to archiving and plotting" eps_sym=final_symmetry symmetry_target=symmetry_target
        end
    end
    return nothing
end
```

The domain is split at the interface $y=0.5$.
* **Density ($\rho$):** A smoothed interface between $\rho_{heavy} = 2.0$ (bottom) and $\rho_{light} = 1.0$ (top).
    $$ \rho(y) = \rho_{heavy} + (\rho_{light} - \rho_{heavy}) \cdot \frac{1}{2}\left(1 + \tanh\left(50(y - 0.5)\right)\right) $$
* **Pressure ($p$):** Hydrostatic balance ($\frac{dp}{dy} = \rho g$).
    $$ p(y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1 & \text{if } y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5 & \text{if } y \geq 0.5 \end{cases} $$
* **Velocity:** Initialized with zero horizontal velocity ($u=0$) and a perturbation in vertical velocity ($v$):
    $$ v(x,y) = -0.025 \cdot c \cdot \cos(8\pi x) \cdot \sin(\pi y)^6 $$
    Where $c = \sqrt{\gamma p / \rho}$ is the local speed of sound.

## Section 3: Forensic Analysis & Feedback

### Observation

**Plot quality and physics:**

* At \(t=0.5\) and \(t=1.0\), the initially smooth interface develops the expected localized single-mode displacement while remaining nearly undisturbed away from the perturbation.
* At \(t=2.0\), the density field shows a well-developed central spike with a mushroom cap and two Kelvin–Helmholtz roll-ups. The vertical-velocity panel consistently shows the central jet and surrounding return flow.
* At \(t=3.0\), the plume has elongated and developed secondary structures while remaining visually mirror-symmetric. The archived spike tip, \(y\approx0.9351\), is consistent with the plot and shows that the spike approaches but does not penetrate the upper wall.
* Interfaces and roll-up structures remain crisp. There is no visible Gibbs ringing, widespread interface smearing, blank panel, or other obvious numerical artifact.

**Verification diagnostics:**

* Relative mass drift is only \(4.51\times10^{-12}\).
* Energy closure is approximately \(3.01\times10^{-9}\).
* Density and pressure remain positive throughout the reported checks.
* Maximum wall-normal velocity at the walls is \(3.63\times10^{-4}\), within the stated wall-compliance expectation.
* Initial-condition and source symmetry are both approximately \(7.99\times10^{-15}\), and the mirror-paired AMR hard assertion completed successfully.
* Final symmetry improved from \(6.27\times10^{-3}\) previously to
  \[
  \varepsilon_{\mathrm{sym}}(3)=2.437921\times10^{-8},
  \]
  but this still exceeds the required \(10^{-10}\) target.

**Error-log interpretation:** The final `Minimum achieved error:` entry is explicitly identified by the log as a **mass-conservation diagnostic only**, not an error against an exact RTI solution. **Quantitative error is not applicable as no ground truth is available.**

### Diagnosis

The primary defect is **residual mirror-symmetry breaking**, not instability, excessive dissipation, or poor boundary treatment.

Mirror-paired AMR was highly effective. Because the initial projection and gravity source are symmetric to roundoff and the mesh-pairing assertion passed, the remaining error likely originates from either:

1. the common-grid interpolation or its mirror indexing;
2. remaining unsymmetrized operations, such as shock-capturing indicator/blending evaluation or AMR transfer operations; or
3. floating-point operation-order differences whose effects are amplified by the physically unstable RTI evolution.

The present evidence does not justify changing HLLC/Ranocha, adding global dissipation, or increasing shock-capturing strength.

### Targeted Improvements

* **For Integrity:** None. The simulation completed successfully, produced readable plots, and passed positivity and archive checks.

* **For Accuracy:**
  1. Report symmetry separately on the **raw DG representation** and the **\(256\times1024\) common grid**, at every save time and ideally immediately before and after AMR. If the raw solution is substantially more symmetric, correct the comparison-grid interpolation or mirror-index mapping rather than the PDE solver.
  2. If the raw DG state carries the same residual, inspect the shock-capturing indicator/blending coefficient and AMR projection/prolongation paths for mirror-paired decisions and consistent operation ordering. Continue the prohibition on averaging or symmetrizing the solution state itself.
  3. Execute the scheduled **polydeg = 6, CFL = 0.25** confirmation run with the same base mesh and AMR policy. Compare relative \(L_2\) density differences and spike-tip positions at \(t=2\) and \(t=3\) against the archived degree-5 reference. If these comparisons do not demonstrate convergence, use a degree-7 confirmation with a suitably smaller CFL rather than aggressive mesh refinement.

* **For Details:** None for the visible solution quality. The degree-6 comparison remains necessary for certification as converged PINN ground truth.

* **For Optimality:** None. DGSEM with HLLC, Ranocha volume flux, selective blending, and density-based AMR remains an appropriate architecture. No exact analytical solution is available for this nonlinear, source-driven, two-dimensional instability with Kelvin–Helmholtz roll-up, so the exact-method override is not triggered.

--- Strategy Report Received ---
# Scientific Strategy Report — ATHENA Lab

**Classification:** Refinement/Certification Iteration (Run 34+) — continuing from the 99/100 DGSEM baseline.

---

**1. Problem Statement**

We solve the single-mode, inviscid, compressible Rayleigh–Taylor instability (RTI): a heavy fluid ($\rho=2$) lies beneath a light fluid ($\rho=1$) with gravity acting in $+y$, an unstable stratification ($\nabla p \cdot \nabla \rho < 0$ across the interface). The prior DGSEM run is stable, positive, mass-conserving to $10^{-12}$, and physically correct, but **certification is incomplete**: (i) the mirror-symmetry residual is $2.44\times10^{-8}$ versus the required $10^{-10}$, and (ii) degree-independent ground-truth convergence is unconfirmed. This iteration executes the Advisor-mandated **polydeg=6, CFL=0.25 confirmation run** with targeted symmetry-forensic diagnostics, escalating to polydeg=7 only if convergence is not demonstrated.

**1. Mathematical Specification (Code-Ready Extraction)**

* **Governing Equations:** 2D Compressible Euler with gravitational source:
$$\partial_t \begin{pmatrix} \rho \\ \rho u \\ \rho v \\ E \end{pmatrix} + \partial_x \begin{pmatrix} \rho u \\ \rho u^2 + p \\ \rho u v \\ u(E+p) \end{pmatrix} + \partial_y \begin{pmatrix} \rho v \\ \rho u v \\ \rho v^2 + p \\ v(E+p) \end{pmatrix} = \begin{pmatrix} 0 \\ 0 \\ \rho g \\ \rho g v \end{pmatrix}$$
with equation of state $p = (\gamma - 1)\left(E - \tfrac{1}{2}\rho(u^2 + v^2)\right)$.

* **Domain:** $(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$, $\quad t \in [0, 3.0]$.

* **Boundary Conditions:**
  * $x = 0$ and $x = 0.25$: **Periodic**.
  * $y = 0$ and $y = 1$: **Reflective / free-slip walls** (no-penetration: $v = 0$).

* **Initial Conditions** (exact formulas, every field):
$$\rho(x,y) = 2.0 + (1.0 - 2.0)\cdot\tfrac{1}{2}\Big(1 + \tanh\big(50(y - 0.5)\big)\Big)$$
$$p(y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1, & y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5, & y \geq 0.5 \end{cases}$$
$$u(x,y) = 0$$
$$v(x,y) = -0.025\; c(y)\, \cos(8\pi x)\, \sin^6(\pi y), \qquad c(y) = \sqrt{\frac{\gamma\, p(y)}{\rho(y)}}$$
$$E(x,y) = \frac{p(y)}{\gamma - 1} + \frac{1}{2}\,\rho(x,y)\, v(x,y)^2$$

* **Constants & Parameters:** $\gamma = 1.4$; gravity vector $\mathbf{g} = [0, 1.0]^T$ (source adds $+\rho g$ to $y$-momentum and $\rho g v$ to energy); $\mu = 0$; $k = 0$.

* **Expected Physics:** Single-mode growth localized at the interface for $t \lesssim 1$; by $t \approx 2$ a central heavy-fluid spike with mushroom cap and two Kelvin–Helmholtz roll-ups; by $t = 3$ an elongated plume with secondary structures, spike tip at $y \approx 0.93$–$0.94$ (approaching, not penetrating, the upper wall). The solution must remain **mirror-symmetric about $x = 0.125$** to $\leq 10^{-10}$.

**2. Strategic Methodology**

* **Can we obtain an exact solution? No — infeasible.** The Method of Characteristics is tractable only for 1D homogeneous hyperbolic systems (simple waves / Riemann invariants). This problem is a **2D nonlinear system with a gravitational source term, wall boundaries, and an unstable interface** whose evolution involves nonlinear mode coupling, KH roll-up, and secondary instabilities. Linear stability theory yields only the early-time growth rate, not the evolution to $t=3$. The Advisor has explicitly confirmed the exact-method override is **not triggered**. Therefore a certified high-order numerical ground truth is the objective.
* **Selection: Discontinuous Galerkin Spectral Element Method in 2D plus time.** Justification: (i) wall boundaries in $y$ exclude Fourier spectral methods; (ii) sharp material interfaces, KH roll-ups, and compressive structures demand local shock capturing and $h$-adaptivity; (iii) the entropy-stable DGSEM architecture has already demonstrated stability, positivity, $10^{-12}$-level mass conservation, and correct RTI morphology in prior runs. The remaining gaps are **verification defects, not architecture defects** — they are addressed by the Advisor's diagnostic and degree-escalation protocol, not by changing fluxes or adding dissipation.

**3. Implementation Requirements (CRITICAL)**

* **Configuration:** Use template defaults **EXCEPT** for the Advisor-mandated overrides below. Retain the same base mesh and AMR policy as the archived degree-5 run.

* **3.0.1 Adaptive Mesh Refinement & Shock Capturing — Variable Selection Analysis:**
  * **AMR indicator variable: Density ($\rho$).** The structural features of RTI — heavy/light interface, spike, mushroom cap, roll-up boundaries, secondary structures — are material-interface phenomena uniquely and sharply marked by density, which remains localized at the features of interest. **Velocity must be avoided:** after roll-up onset ($t \gtrsim 2$), $v$ becomes globally chaotic across the entire mixing region, so velocity-based refinement would waste resources refining everywhere. **Pressure must be avoided:** it is dominated by the smooth hydrostatic stratification ($dp/dy = \rho g$) and would smear refinement vertically over the whole domain. Density is also the archived ground-truth comparison field and feeds the indicator with a bounded, high-contrast signal ($1 \leq \rho \leq 2$).
  * **Shock capturing:** density-based indicator with selective blending, strength unchanged (Advisor: evidence does not justify increasing it, changing HLLC/Ranocha fluxes, or adding global dissipation).
  * **Mirror-paired AMR (retain):** all AMR decisions made symmetrically about $x = 0.125$, with the post-rebalance hard assertion that every leaf has an identical-size/level mirror partner.

* **3.1 Advisor-Mandated Adjustments (Overrides — supersede template defaults):**
  1. **Degree-6 confirmation run:** polydeg = **6** (from 5), CFL = **0.25** (from 0.3), same base mesh and AMR policy.
  2. **Dual symmetry diagnostics:** report the mirror-symmetry residual $\varepsilon_{\mathrm{sym}}$ **separately on (a) the raw DG representation and (b) the $256\times1024$ common comparison grid**, at **every save time** and **immediately before and after each AMR operation**. Target: $\varepsilon_{\mathrm{sym}}(3) \leq 10^{-10}$.
  3. **Conditional repair directives:** if the raw DG state is substantially more symmetric than the common-grid value, fix the comparison-grid interpolation or mirror-index mapping — **not** the PDE solver. If the raw DG state carries the same residual, inspect the shock-capturing indicator/blending evaluation and AMR projection/prolongation paths for mirror-paired decisions and consistent floating-point operation ordering.
  4. **Prohibition (retain):** no averaging or symmetrization of the solution state itself.
  5. **Degree-5 archive comparison:** load the archived degree-5 NPZ reference; compute and print relative $L_2$ density differences and spike-tip position deltas at $t = 2$ and $t = 3$.
  6. **Escalation rule:** if the degree-6 vs. degree-5 comparison does not demonstrate convergence, execute a **polydeg = 7** run with a suitably smaller CFL — **not** aggressive mesh refinement.
  7. **Retain all integrity diagnostics:** relative mass drift, positivity of $\rho$ and $p$, wall no-penetration compliance, cumulative gravity-work energy balance, initial-condition and source symmetry checks (roundoff, $\sim 10^{-15}$, with logging/abort thresholds), and the final verification summary beginning with `Minimum achieved error:` (explicitly labeled as a mass-conservation diagnostic).

**4. Visualization Strategy**

* **Field Selection (Analysis):** Plot **Density ($\rho$)** and **vertical velocity ($v$)** only. $\rho$ is the primary driver of the instability (Atwood-number buoyancy) and marks the interface, spike, and mushroom structures; $v$ captures the dominant dynamics (central jet ascent and surrounding return flow). Do not plot $u$, $p$, or $E$ — additional panels would be too small to read.
* **Task:** Plot $\rho$ and $v$ at $t = 0.5,\ 1.0,\ 2.0,\ 3.0$.
* **Layout:** Grid — Rows = [$\rho$, $v$], Columns = [4 time steps].
* **Saving Data:** Archive the **full solution state** (all variables: primitive plot data, raw DG conserved state, common-grid interpolated conservative data, metadata, spike-tip positions) at `dt_save = 0.1` for Ground Truth.
* **Constraints:** Save as `summary_all.png` (strictly). **CRITICAL: disable colorbars (`colorbar=false`) — Julia.** No fixed colormaps; let scales adjust automatically. Plot only primary variables; no derived quantities (no vorticity).

**5. Cross-Validation & Data Integration (Conditional)**

* No external sensor/reference paths are present in the problem statement. However, per the Advisor mandate, cross-validation against the **archived degree-5 reference** (prior run's NPZ archive, `dt_save=0.1`) is required:
  * Load the archived degree-5 NPZ files.
  * Compute and print the **Relative $L_2$ density difference** at $t = 2.0$ and $t = 3.0$ between the new degree-6 solution and the degree-5 archive.
  * Compute and print the **spike-tip position deltas** at $t = 2.0$ and $t = 3.0$.
  * **Certification criteria:** (i) $\varepsilon_{\mathrm{sym}}(3) \leq 10^{-10}$ on both raw DG and common grid; (ii) degree-independent agreement in the above metrics; (iii) all integrity diagnostics pass. If (ii) fails, escalate per §3.1 item 6.

```julia
# The standard callback still performs all projection, rebalancing, and
# reinitialization. The wrapper adds read-only symmetry forensics around each
# adaptation and retains the mandatory post-rebalance topology assertion.
function mirror_paired_amr_affect!(integrator)
    report_amr_symmetry!(integrator, "before AMR")
    base_amr_callback.affect!(integrator)
    mirror_amr_leaf_partners(integrator.p)
    report_amr_symmetry!(integrator, "after AMR")
    return nothing
end
function mirror_paired_amr_initialize(callback, u, t, integrator)
    report_amr_symmetry!(integrator, "before initial AMR")
    if base_amr_callback.initialize !== nothing
        base_amr_callback.initialize(base_amr_callback, u, t, integrator)
    end
    mirror_amr_leaf_partners(integrator.p)
    report_amr_symmetry!(integrator, "after initial AMR")
    return nothing
end
amr_callback = DiscreteCallback(base_amr_callback.condition,
                                mirror_paired_amr_affect!;
                                initialize=mirror_paired_amr_initialize,
                                save_positions=base_amr_callback.save_positions)
```

```julia
function raw_dg_mirror_symmetry_error(integrator)
    semi_local = integrator.p
    state = Trixi.wrap_array(integrator.u, semi_local)
    coordinates = semi_local.cache.elements.node_coordinates
    ndims(state) == 4 && size(state, 1) == 4 ||
        error("Raw symmetry diagnostic: expected a four-variable 2D DG state")
    ndims(coordinates) == 4 && size(coordinates, 1) == 2 ||
        error("Raw symmetry diagnostic: invalid node-coordinate storage")
    size(state, 2) == size(coordinates, 2) &&
        size(state, 3) == size(coordinates, 3) &&
        size(state, 4) == size(coordinates, 4) ||
        error("Raw symmetry diagnostic: inconsistent state and coordinate layouts")

    partners = mirror_amr_leaf_partners(semi_local)
    numerator = 0.0
    denominator = 0.0
    for element in axes(state, 4)
        partner = partners[element]
        for j in axes(state, 3), i in axes(state, 2)
            x = coordinates[1, i, j, element]
            y = coordinates[2, i, j, element]
            target_x = 0.25 - x
            mirror_i = 0
            mirror_j = 0
            best_distance = Inf
            # Match local nodes geometrically without assuming p4est's node
            # orientation. This only reads the unmodified conservative state.
            for jj in axes(state, 3), ii in axes(state, 2)
                distance =
                    (coordinates[1, ii, jj, partner] - target_x)^2 +
                    (coordinates[2, ii, jj, partner] - y)^2
                if distance < best_distance
                    best_distance = distance
                    mirror_i = ii
                    mirror_j = jj
                end
            end
            best_distance <= 1.0e-24 ||
                error("Raw symmetry diagnostic: no matching mirror DG node " *
                      "for element $element, local node ($i,$j)")
            for variable in 1:4
                value = state[variable, i, j, element]
                mirror_value =
                    state[variable, mirror_i, mirror_j, partner]
                # rho, rho_v, and E are even; rho_u is odd under reflection.
                residual = variable == 2 ? value + mirror_value :
                           value - mirror_value
                numerator += residual^2
                denominator += value^2
            end
        end
    end
    denominator > 0.0 || return NaN
    return sqrt(numerator / denominator)
end
function interpolate_conserved_grid(x, y, fields;
```

```julia
function report_amr_symmetry!(integrator, phase)
    raw_x, raw_y, raw_conserved = raw_dg_state(integrator)
    grid_fields = interpolate_conserved_grid(raw_x, raw_y, raw_conserved)
    raw_symmetry = raw_dg_mirror_symmetry_error(integrator)
    grid_symmetry = mirror_symmetry_error(grid_fields)
    verification_latest[:amr_raw_symmetry] = raw_symmetry
    verification_latest[:amr_grid_symmetry] = grid_symmetry
    println(Printf.@sprintf(
        "AMR symmetry %s at t=%.8f: eps_raw=%.6e, eps_grid=%.6e",
        phase, integrator.t, raw_symmetry, grid_symmetry))
    return nothing
end
```

```julia
function finalize_symmetry_verification(callback, u, t, integrator)
    if integrator.t >= 3.0 - 8 * eps(3.0)
        raw_x, raw_y, raw_conserved = raw_dg_state(integrator)
        grid_fields = interpolate_conserved_grid(raw_x, raw_y, raw_conserved)
        final_raw_symmetry = raw_dg_mirror_symmetry_error(integrator)
        final_grid_symmetry = mirror_symmetry_error(grid_fields)

        verification_latest[:time] = integrator.t
        # Retain :symmetry as the common-grid value for compatibility.
        verification_latest[:symmetry] = final_grid_symmetry
        verification_latest[:raw_symmetry] = final_raw_symmetry
        verification_latest[:grid_symmetry] = final_grid_symmetry
        verification_latest[:final_symmetry] = final_grid_symmetry
        verification_latest[:final_raw_symmetry] = final_raw_symmetry
        verification_latest[:final_grid_symmetry] = final_grid_symmetry
        symmetry_target = 1.0e-10
        verification_latest[:symmetry_target] = symmetry_target
        println(Printf.@sprintf(
            "Final mirror symmetry at t=%.5f: eps_sym_raw=%.6e, eps_sym_grid=%.6e (diagnostic target <= %.1e)",
            integrator.t, final_raw_symmetry, final_grid_symmetry,
            symmetry_target))

        # Report both diagnostics without aborting a completed simulation.
        # The solution itself is never averaged or symmetrized.
        for (representation, residual) in
            (("raw DG", final_raw_symmetry),
             ("common grid", final_grid_symmetry))
            if !isfinite(residual)
                @warn "Final mirror-symmetry diagnostic is non-finite; continuing to finalization" representation=representation eps_sym=residual
            elseif residual > symmetry_target
                @warn "Final mirror-symmetry diagnostic exceeds the target; continuing to archiving and plotting" representation=representation eps_sym=residual symmetry_target=symmetry_target
            end
        end
    end
    return nothing
end
```

```julia
function compare_degree5_archive(final_data)
    reference_directory = joinpath(@__DIR__, "data_npz_polydeg5")
    comparisons = NamedTuple[]

    for target_time in (2.0, 3.0)
        if target_time == 3.0
            current_data = final_data
        else
            current_filename = joinpath(
                npz_out, Printf.@sprintf("time_%08.5f.npz", target_time))
            verify_npz_file(current_filename)
            current_data = npzread(current_filename)
        end

        reference_filename = joinpath(
            reference_directory,
            Printf.@sprintf("time_%08.5f.npz", target_time))
        isfile(reference_filename) ||
            error("Degree-5 cross-validation: missing archive $reference_filename")
        reference_data = npzread(reference_filename)
        required_keys = ("t", "polydeg", "grid_rho", "spike_tip",
                         "comparison_x", "comparison_y")
        all(key -> haskey(reference_data, key), required_keys) ||
            error("Degree-5 cross-validation: incomplete archive " *
                  "$reference_filename")
        Int(npz_scalar(reference_data["polydeg"])) == 5 ||
            error("Degree-5 cross-validation: reference is not degree 5")
        isapprox(Float64(npz_scalar(reference_data["t"])), target_time;
                 atol=1.0e-10, rtol=0.0) ||
            error("Degree-5 cross-validation: noncommon reference time")

        current_rho = Float64.(current_data["grid_rho"])
        reference_rho = Float64.(reference_data["grid_rho"])
        size(current_rho) == (comparison_ny, comparison_nx) &&
            size(reference_rho) == size(current_rho) ||
            error("Degree-5 cross-validation: common-grid density layouts differ")
        length(reference_data["comparison_x"]) == comparison_nx &&
            length(reference_data["comparison_y"]) == comparison_ny ||
            error("Degree-5 cross-validation: inconsistent comparison axes")
        all(isfinite, current_rho) && all(isfinite, reference_rho) ||
            error("Degree-5 cross-validation: non-finite density data")

        denominator = sum(abs2, reference_rho)
        denominator > 0.0 ||
            error("Degree-5 cross-validation: zero reference density norm")
        relative_l2 = sqrt(sum(abs2, current_rho .- reference_rho) /
                           denominator)
        current_tip = Float64(npz_scalar(current_data["spike_tip"]))
        reference_tip = Float64(npz_scalar(reference_data["spike_tip"]))
        isfinite(relative_l2) && isfinite(current_tip) &&
            isfinite(reference_tip) ||
            error("Degree-5 cross-validation: non-finite comparison metric")
        spike_delta = current_tip - reference_tip

        push!(comparisons,
              (time=target_time, relative_l2=relative_l2,
               spike_delta=spike_delta))
        println(Printf.@sprintf(
            "Degree-%d/degree-5 comparison at t=%.1f: relative_L2_rho=%.6e, spike_tip_current=%.6e, spike_tip_degree5=%.6e, delta_spike_tip=%.6e, abs_delta_spike_tip=%.6e",
            polydeg, target_time, relative_l2, current_tip, reference_tip,
            spike_delta, abs(spike_delta)))
    end

    # Screening tolerances for the otherwise unquantified escalation rule.
    # They affect certification only, never the PDE solution. The spike
    # tolerance corresponds to two common-grid rows.
    relative_l2_tolerance = 1.0e-2
    spike_tip_tolerance = 2.0 / comparison_ny
    degree_agreement = all(
        comparison -> comparison.relative_l2 <= relative_l2_tolerance &&
                      abs(comparison.spike_delta) <= spike_tip_tolerance,
        comparisons)
    println(Printf.@sprintf(
        "Degree-agreement decision: %s; both times require relative_L2_rho <= %.1e and abs_delta_spike_tip <= %.6e",
        degree_agreement ? "demonstrated" : "not demonstrated",
        relative_l2_tolerance, spike_tip_tolerance))
    return degree_agreement
end
```
